\displaystyle\tilde{A}_{1}=\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}+\big(\tilde{y}+\frac{1}{\tilde{y}}\big)\textrm{erf}\big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\big);

\displaystyle\tilde{A}_{3}=\frac{\textrm{erf}(\tilde{y}/\sqrt{2})}{5\tilde{y}^{3}}-\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{5\tilde{y}^{2}};

\displaystyle\tilde{A}_{5}=-\,\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{140},

(K11)

so that the second derivative calculates easily

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial\boldsymbol{v}}\frac{\partial G_{b}}{\partial\boldsymbol{v}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{b}\sqrt{\frac{m_{b}}{T_{b}}}\bigg\{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}\frac{\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}+\frac{\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}}{\tilde{y}^{2}}\Big(\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}\Big)

(K12)

\displaystyle+\Big(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)}+\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}+\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)})\Big)\frac{\tilde{A}_{3}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}+\frac{\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}}{\tilde{y}^{2}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)})\Big(\tilde{A}_{3}^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\tilde{A}_{3}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)}+\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}+\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)})\Big)\frac{\tilde{A}_{5}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}+\frac{\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}}{\tilde{y}^{2}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)})\Big(\tilde{A}_{5}^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\tilde{A}_{5}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}\Big)\bigg\},

and applying
(1/2)\textrm{Tr}
recovers
H_{b}
. The coefficients are

\displaystyle\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{\tilde{y}}+\big(1-\frac{1}{\tilde{y}^{2}}\big)\textrm{erf}\big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\big);

\displaystyle\tilde{A}_{3}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\big(\frac{1}{\tilde{y}}+\frac{3}{\tilde{y}^{3}}\big)\frac{e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{5}-\frac{3}{5\tilde{y}^{4}}\textrm{erf}\big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\big);

\displaystyle\tilde{A}_{5}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{\tilde{y}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{140};

\displaystyle\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{2}{\tilde{y}^{2}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}+\frac{2}{\tilde{y}^{3}}\textrm{erf}\big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\big);

\displaystyle\tilde{A}_{3}^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\big(1+\frac{4}{\tilde{y}^{2}}+\frac{12}{\tilde{y}^{4}}\big)\frac{e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{5}+\frac{12}{5\tilde{y}^{5}}\textrm{erf}\big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\big);

\displaystyle\tilde{A}_{5}^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}(\tilde{y}^{2}-1)\frac{e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{140},

(K13)

and so

\displaystyle\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{3}{\tilde{y}^{2}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}-\big(\frac{1}{\tilde{y}}-\frac{3}{\tilde{y}^{3}}\big)\textrm{erf}\big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\big);

\displaystyle\tilde{A}_{3}^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\tilde{A}_{3}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\big(1+\frac{5}{\tilde{y}^{2}}+\frac{15}{\tilde{y}^{4}}\big)\frac{e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{5}+\frac{3}{\tilde{y}^{5}}\textrm{erf}\big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\big);

\displaystyle\tilde{A}_{5}^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\tilde{A}_{5}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{\tilde{y}^{2}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{140}.

(K14)

K.2
Dynamical friction vector and diffusion tensor

The dynamical friction vector thus reads

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{A}^{ab}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\frac{c_{ab}}{m_{a}^{2}}\big(1+\frac{m_{a}}{m_{b}}\big)\frac{n_{b}m_{b}}{T_{b}}\Big[\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\Big(\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{\tilde{y}^{2}}-\frac{\textrm{erf}(\tilde{y}/\sqrt{2})}{\tilde{y}^{3}}\Big)

(K15)

\displaystyle-\,\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{10}\Big(\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)}-\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)})+(\tilde{y}^{2}-5)\frac{\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)}}{28}-(\tilde{y}^{2}-7)\frac{\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)})}{28}\Big)\Big],

and the diffusion tensor

\displaystyle\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}^{ab}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\frac{c_{ab}}{m_{a}^{2}}n_{b}\sqrt{\frac{m_{b}}{T_{b}}}\bigg\{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}\frac{\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}+\frac{\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}}{\tilde{y}^{2}}\Big(\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}\Big)

(K16)

\displaystyle+\Big(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)}+\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}+\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)})\Big)\frac{\tilde{A}_{3}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}+\frac{\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}}{\tilde{y}^{2}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)})\Big(\tilde{A}_{3}^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\tilde{A}_{3}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)}+\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}+\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)})\Big)\frac{\tilde{A}_{5}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}+\frac{\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}}{\tilde{y}^{2}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)})\Big(\tilde{A}_{5}^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\tilde{A}_{5}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}\Big)\bigg\},

or in its entire beauty

\displaystyle\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}^{ab}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\frac{c_{ab}}{m_{a}^{2}}n_{b}\sqrt{\frac{m_{b}}{T_{b}}}\bigg\{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}\Big[\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{\tilde{y}^{2}}+\big(\frac{1}{\tilde{y}}-\frac{1}{\tilde{y}^{3}}\big)\textrm{erf}\big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\big)\Big]

(K17)

\displaystyle+\frac{\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}}{\tilde{y}^{2}}\Big[-\,\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{3}{\tilde{y}^{2}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}-\big(\frac{1}{\tilde{y}}-\frac{3}{\tilde{y}^{3}}\big)\textrm{erf}\big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\big)\Big]

\displaystyle+\Big(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)}+\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}+\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)})\Big)\Big[\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\big(\frac{1}{\tilde{y}^{2}}+\frac{3}{\tilde{y}^{4}}\big)\frac{e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{5}-\frac{3}{5\tilde{y}^{5}}\textrm{erf}\big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\big)\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}}{\tilde{y}^{2}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)})\Big[-\,\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\big(1+\frac{5}{\tilde{y}^{2}}+\frac{15}{\tilde{y}^{4}}\big)\frac{e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{5}+\frac{3}{\tilde{y}^{5}}\textrm{erf}\big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\big)\Big]

\displaystyle+\Big(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)}+\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}+\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)})\Big)\Big[\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{140}\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}}{\tilde{y}^{2}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)})\Big[-\,\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{\tilde{y}^{2}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{140}\Big]\bigg\}.

As a reminder

\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}=\sqrt{\frac{m_{b}}{T_{b}}}(\boldsymbol{v}-\boldsymbol{u}_{b});\qquad c_{ab}=2\pi e^{4}Z_{a}^{2}Z_{b}^{2}\ln\Lambda

(K18)

K.3
Distribution function for species “a”

The general distribution function for species “a” reads

f_{a}=f^{(0)}_{a}(1+\chi_{a})=n_{a}\Big(\frac{m_{a}}{T_{a}}\Big)^{3/2}\phi^{(0)}_{a}(1+\chi_{a});\qquad\phi^{(0)}_{a}=\frac{e^{-\tilde{c}^{2}_{a}/2}}{(2\pi)^{3/2}};\qquad\tilde{c}_{a}=\sqrt{\frac{m_{a}}{T_{a}}}(\boldsymbol{v}-\boldsymbol{u}_{a}),

(K19)

where the perturbation

\chi_{a}=\frac{1}{10}\tilde{h}_{i}^{a(3)}\tilde{H}_{i}^{a(3)}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{c}}_{a})+\frac{1}{280}\tilde{h}_{i}^{a(5)}\tilde{H}_{i}^{a(5)}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{c}}_{a}).

(K20)

To avoid the complicated run-away effect, the weight has to be expanded with small drifts, for example
by defining

\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}}=(\boldsymbol{u}_{b}-\boldsymbol{u}_{a})\sqrt{\frac{m_{a}}{T_{a}}};\qquad\alpha=\frac{\sqrt{T_{b}/m_{b}}}{\sqrt{T_{a}/m_{a}}};\qquad\tilde{\boldsymbol{c}}_{a}=\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}+\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}},

(K21)

so that the expansion for small drifts

e^{-\tilde{c}_{a}^{2}/2}=e^{-|\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}+\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}}|^{2}/2}\simeq e^{-\alpha^{2}\tilde{y}^{2}/2}\big(1-\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}}\big).

(K22)

In comparison to our previously used normalization
\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}=\boldsymbol{y}\sqrt{2}
and
\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}}=\boldsymbol{u}\sqrt{2}
and
\tilde{\boldsymbol{c}}_{a}=\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\sqrt{2}/v_{\textrm{th}a}
.
The perturbation
\chi_{a}
contains Hermite polynomials, and these also have to be expanded in the semi-linear approximation.
Importantly, after contraction with Hermite (fluid) moments

\displaystyle\tilde{h}^{a(3)}_{i}\tilde{H}^{a(3)}_{i}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{c}}_{a})\simeq\tilde{h}^{a(3)}_{i}\alpha\tilde{y}_{i}(\alpha^{2}\tilde{y}^{2}-5)=\tilde{h}^{a(3)}_{i}\tilde{H}^{a(3)}_{i}(\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}});

\displaystyle\tilde{h}^{a(5)}_{i}\tilde{H}^{a(5)}_{i}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{c}}_{a})\simeq\tilde{h}^{a(5)}_{i}\alpha\tilde{y}_{i}(\alpha^{4}\tilde{y}^{4}-14\alpha^{2}\tilde{y}^{2}+35)=\tilde{h}^{a(5)}_{i}\tilde{H}^{a(5)}_{i}(\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}),

(K23)

where all the drift
\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}}
contributions such as
\tilde{h}^{a(3)}_{i}\tilde{u}_{i}
are neglected in the semi-linear approximation.
The expanded distribution function thus reads

f_{a}=n_{a}\Big(\frac{m_{a}}{T_{a}}\Big)^{3/2}\frac{e^{-\alpha^{2}\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{(2\pi)^{3/2}}(1-\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}}+\chi_{a}),

(K24)

with perturbation

\chi_{a}=\frac{1}{10}\tilde{h}_{i}^{a(3)}\tilde{H}_{i}^{a(3)}(\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})+\frac{1}{280}\tilde{h}_{i}^{a(5)}\tilde{H}_{i}^{a(5)}(\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}).

(K25)

Integrals are evaluated with
d^{3}v=(T_{b}/m_{b})^{3/2}d^{3}\tilde{y}
, so a useful shortcut is

\int f_{a}d^{3}v=n_{a}\alpha^{3}\int\frac{e^{-\alpha^{2}\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{(2\pi)^{3/2}}(1-\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}}+\chi_{a})d^{3}\tilde{y}.

(K26)

Also, it is useful to express
c_{ab}
directly through the collisional frequencies
\nu_{ab}
, according to

2\frac{c_{ab}n_{b}}{m_{a}^{2}}\big(1+\frac{m_{a}}{m_{b}}\big)=3\nu_{ab}\sqrt{\frac{\pi}{2}}(1+\alpha^{2})^{3/2}\Big(\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}\Big)^{3/2}.

(K27)

K.4
Momentum exchange rates
R_{ab}

The momentum exchange rates calculate

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{R}_{ab}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle m_{a}\int f_{a}\boldsymbol{A}^{ab}d^{3}v

(K28)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\nu_{ab}\rho_{a}\sqrt{\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}}+\frac{3}{5}\nu_{ab}\frac{\mu_{ab}}{T_{ab}}\Big[\frac{p_{a}}{2}\sqrt{\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{a(3)}-\frac{\rho_{a}}{\rho_{b}}\frac{p_{b}}{2}\sqrt{\frac{T_{b}}{m_{b}}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{56}\nu_{ab}\Big(\frac{\mu_{ab}}{T_{ab}}\Big)^{2}\Big[p_{a}\Big(\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}\Big)^{3/2}\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{a(5)}-\frac{\rho_{a}}{\rho_{b}}p_{b}\Big(\frac{T_{b}}{m_{b}}\Big)^{3/2}\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)}\Big],

or expressed through usual fluid variables

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{R}_{ab}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\nu_{ab}\rho_{a}(\boldsymbol{u}_{b}-\boldsymbol{u}_{a})+\frac{3}{5}\nu_{ab}\frac{\mu_{ab}}{T_{ab}}\Big[\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}^{a}-\frac{\rho_{a}}{\rho_{b}}\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}^{b}\Big]

(K29)

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{56}\nu_{ab}\Big(\frac{\mu_{ab}}{T_{ab}}\Big)^{2}\Big[\Big(\vec{\boldsymbol{X}}^{a(5)}-28\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}^{a}\Big)-\frac{\rho_{a}}{\rho_{b}}\Big(\vec{\boldsymbol{X}}^{b(5)}-28\frac{T_{b}}{m_{b}}\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}^{b}\Big)\Big].

Note that
\boldsymbol{R}_{ab}=-\boldsymbol{R}_{ba}
. An alternative form reads

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{R}_{ab}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\nu_{ab}\rho_{a}(\boldsymbol{u}_{b}-\boldsymbol{u}_{a})+\nu_{ab}\frac{\mu_{ab}}{T_{ab}}\Big[\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}^{a}\Big(\frac{3}{5}+\frac{3}{2}\frac{\mu_{ab}}{m_{a}}\frac{T_{a}}{T_{ab}}\Big)-\frac{\rho_{a}}{\rho_{b}}\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}^{b}\Big(\frac{3}{5}+\frac{3}{2}\frac{\mu_{ab}}{m_{b}}\frac{T_{b}}{T_{ab}}\Big)\Big]

(K30)

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{56}\nu_{ab}\Big(\frac{\mu_{ab}}{T_{ab}}\Big)^{2}\Big[\vec{\boldsymbol{X}}^{a(5)}-\frac{\rho_{a}}{\rho_{b}}\vec{\boldsymbol{X}}^{b(5)}\Big],

or yet another one

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{R}_{ab}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\nu_{ab}\rho_{a}(\boldsymbol{u}_{b}-\boldsymbol{u}_{a})+\nu_{ab}\frac{\mu_{ab}}{T_{ab}}\Big[\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}^{a}\frac{\frac{21}{10}T_{a}m_{b}+\frac{3}{5}T_{b}m_{a}}{T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a}}-\frac{\rho_{a}}{\rho_{b}}\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}^{b}\frac{\frac{3}{5}T_{a}m_{b}+\frac{21}{10}T_{b}m_{a}}{T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a}}\Big]

(K31)

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{56}\nu_{ab}\Big(\frac{\mu_{ab}}{T_{ab}}\Big)^{2}\Big[\vec{\boldsymbol{X}}^{a(5)}-\frac{\rho_{a}}{\rho_{b}}\vec{\boldsymbol{X}}^{b(5)}\Big].

K.5
Heat flux exchange rates

We need to calculate collisional contributions for the heat flux

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\textrm{Tr}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}}_{ab}^{(3)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\delta\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}_{ab}}{\delta t}=m_{a}\int f_{a}\Big[(\boldsymbol{A}_{ab}\cdot\boldsymbol{c}_{a})\boldsymbol{c}_{a}+\frac{1}{2}\boldsymbol{A}_{ab}|\boldsymbol{c}_{a}|^{2}\Big]d^{3}v

(K32)

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+m_{a}\int f_{a}\Big[\frac{1}{2}(\textrm{Tr}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}_{ab})\boldsymbol{c}_{a}+\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}_{ab}\cdot\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\Big]d^{3}v,

where the velocity

\boldsymbol{c}_{a}=\sqrt{\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{c}}_{a}=\sqrt{\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}}(\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}+\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}}).

(K33)

Before attempting integration of (
K32
), it is useful to apply the semi-linear approximation, which yields step-by-step

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{A}^{ab}\cdot\boldsymbol{c}_{a}

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle 2\frac{c_{ab}}{m_{a}^{2}}\big(1+\frac{m_{a}}{m_{b}}\big)\frac{n_{b}m_{b}}{T_{b}}\sqrt{\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}}\bigg[(\alpha\tilde{y}^{2}+\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}})\Big(\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{\tilde{y}^{2}}-\frac{\textrm{erf}(\tilde{y}/\sqrt{2})}{\tilde{y}^{3}}\Big)

(K34)

\displaystyle-\,\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{10}\alpha\Big((\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)})(1-\tilde{y}^{2})+\frac{(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)})}{28}(8\tilde{y}^{2}-\tilde{y}^{4}-5)\Big)\bigg];

\displaystyle(\boldsymbol{A}^{ab}\cdot\boldsymbol{c}_{a})\boldsymbol{c}_{a}

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle 2\frac{c_{ab}}{m_{a}^{2}}\big(1+\frac{m_{a}}{m_{b}}\big)\frac{n_{b}m_{b}}{T_{b}}\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}\bigg[

(K35)

\displaystyle+\alpha\Big(\alpha\tilde{y}^{2}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}+\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}})+\tilde{y}^{2}\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}}\Big)\Big(\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{\tilde{y}^{2}}-\frac{\textrm{erf}(\tilde{y}/\sqrt{2})}{\tilde{y}^{3}}\Big)

\displaystyle-\,\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{10}\alpha^{2}\Big(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)})(1-\tilde{y}^{2})+\frac{\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)})}{28}(8\tilde{y}^{2}-\tilde{y}^{4}-5)\Big)\bigg].

Furthermore, in the semi-linear approximation

|\boldsymbol{c}_{a}|^{2}\simeq\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}\big(\alpha^{2}\tilde{y}^{2}+2\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}}\big),

(K36)

and thus

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{A}^{ab}|\boldsymbol{c}_{a}|^{2}

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle 2\frac{c_{ab}}{m_{a}^{2}}\big(1+\frac{m_{a}}{m_{b}}\big)\frac{n_{b}m_{b}}{T_{b}}\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}\bigg[

(K37)

\displaystyle+\alpha\Big(\alpha\tilde{y}^{2}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}+2\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}})\Big)\Big(\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{\tilde{y}^{2}}-\frac{\textrm{erf}(\tilde{y}/\sqrt{2})}{\tilde{y}^{3}}\Big)

\displaystyle-\,\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{10}\alpha^{2}\tilde{y}^{2}\Big(\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)}-\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)})+(\tilde{y}^{2}-5)\frac{\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)}}{28}-(\tilde{y}^{2}-7)\frac{\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)})}{28}\Big)\bigg].

For the diffusion tensor

\displaystyle\textrm{Tr}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}^{ab}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\frac{c_{ab}}{m_{a}^{2}}n_{b}\sqrt{\frac{m_{b}}{T_{b}}}\bigg\{\Big(2\frac{\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}+\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime\prime}\Big)

(K38)

\displaystyle+(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)})\Big(4\frac{\tilde{A}_{3}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}+\tilde{A}_{3}^{\prime\prime}\Big)+(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)})\Big(4\frac{\tilde{A}_{5}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}+\tilde{A}_{5}^{\prime\prime}\Big)\bigg\},

and in the semi-linear approximation

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(\textrm{Tr}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}^{ab})\boldsymbol{c}_{a}

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle 2\frac{c_{ab}}{m_{a}^{2}}\frac{n_{b}}{\alpha}\bigg\{(\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}+\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}})\Big(\frac{\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}+\frac{\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime\prime}}{2}\Big)

(K39)

\displaystyle+\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)})\Big(2\frac{\tilde{A}_{3}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}+\frac{\tilde{A}_{3}^{\prime\prime}}{2}\Big)+\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)})\Big(2\frac{\tilde{A}_{5}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}+\frac{\tilde{A}_{5}^{\prime\prime}}{2}\Big)\bigg\};

\displaystyle\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}^{ab}\cdot\boldsymbol{c}_{a}

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle 2\frac{c_{ab}}{m_{a}^{2}}\frac{n_{b}}{\alpha}\bigg\{\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime\prime}+\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}}\frac{\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}+\frac{\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}})}{\tilde{y}^{2}}\Big(\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}\Big)

(K40)

\displaystyle+\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)}\tilde{y}\tilde{A}_{3}^{\prime}+\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)})\Big(\frac{\tilde{A}_{3}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}+\tilde{A}_{3}^{\prime\prime}\Big)

\displaystyle+\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)}\tilde{y}\tilde{A}_{5}^{\prime}+\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)})\Big(\frac{\tilde{A}_{5}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}+\tilde{A}_{5}^{\prime\prime}\Big)\bigg\}.

Collecting all the results together the first part of (
K32
) becomes

\displaystyle(\boldsymbol{A}^{ab}\cdot\boldsymbol{c}_{a})\boldsymbol{c}_{a}+\frac{1}{2}\boldsymbol{A}^{ab}|\boldsymbol{c}_{a}|^{2}

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle 2\frac{c_{ab}}{m_{a}^{2}}\big(1+\frac{m_{a}}{m_{b}}\big)\frac{n_{b}}{\alpha^{2}}\bigg\{

(K41)

\displaystyle+\alpha\Big(\frac{3}{2}\alpha\tilde{y}^{2}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}+2\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}})+\tilde{y}^{2}\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}}\Big)\Big(\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{\tilde{y}^{2}}-\frac{\textrm{erf}(\tilde{y}/\sqrt{2})}{\tilde{y}^{3}}\Big)

\displaystyle-\,\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{10}\alpha^{2}\Big(\frac{1}{2}\tilde{y}^{2}\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)}+\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)})(1-\frac{3}{2}\tilde{y}^{2})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\tilde{y}^{2}(\tilde{y}^{2}-5)\frac{\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)}}{28}+\frac{\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)})}{28}\big(\frac{23}{2}\tilde{y}^{2}-\frac{3}{2}\tilde{y}^{4}-5\big)\Big)\bigg\};

and the second part of (
K32
) becomes

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(\textrm{Tr}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}^{ab})\boldsymbol{c}_{a}+\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}^{ab}\cdot\boldsymbol{c}_{a}

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle 2\frac{c_{ab}}{m_{a}^{2}}\frac{n_{b}}{\alpha}\bigg\{\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\Big(\frac{\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}+\frac{3\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime\prime}}{2}\Big)+\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}}\Big(2\frac{\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}+\frac{\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime\prime}}{2}\Big)+\frac{\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}})}{\tilde{y}^{2}}\Big(\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}\Big)

(K42)

\displaystyle+\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)}\tilde{y}\tilde{A}_{3}^{\prime}+\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)})\Big(3\frac{\tilde{A}_{3}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}+\frac{3}{2}\tilde{A}_{3}^{\prime\prime}\Big)

\displaystyle+\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)}\tilde{y}\tilde{A}_{5}^{\prime}+\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)})\Big(3\frac{\tilde{A}_{5}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}+\frac{3}{2}\tilde{A}_{5}^{\prime\prime}\Big)\bigg\}.

Now (
K32
) can be directly integrated, by again applying semi-linear approximation during integration.
By using (
K26
) & (
K27
) the entire collisional integral (
K32
) can be written in a symbolic form

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\textrm{Tr}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}}_{ab}^{(3)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle m_{a}n_{a}3\alpha^{3}\nu_{ab}\sqrt{\frac{\pi}{2}}\big(1+\alpha^{2}\big)^{3/2}\Big(\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}\Big)^{3/2}\times

(K43)

\displaystyle\int\frac{e^{-\alpha^{2}\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{(2\pi)^{3/2}}\big(1-\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}}+\chi_{a}\big)\Big[\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}}\{\ref{eq:Thierry13}\}+\frac{1}{(1+\frac{m_{a}}{m_{b}})\alpha}\{\ref{eq:Thierry14}\}\Big]d^{3}\tilde{y},

where {
K41
} & {
K42
} represent only parts of the corresponding equations that are inside of curly brackets.
The final result of integration reads

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\textrm{Tr}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}}_{ab}^{(3)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\delta\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}_{ab}}{\delta t}=-\nu_{ab}p_{a}(\boldsymbol{u}_{b}-\boldsymbol{u}_{a})U_{ab(1)}

(K44)

\displaystyle-\nu_{ab}D_{ab(1)}\frac{p_{a}}{2}\sqrt{\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{a(3)}+\nu_{ab}D_{ab(2)}\frac{\rho_{a}}{\rho_{b}}\frac{p_{b}}{2}\sqrt{\frac{T_{b}}{m_{b}}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)}

\displaystyle+\nu_{ab}E_{ab(1)}p_{a}\sqrt{\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{a(5)}+\nu_{ab}E_{ab(2)}\frac{\rho_{a}}{\rho_{b}}p_{b}\sqrt{\frac{T_{b}}{m_{b}}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)},

with mass-ratio coefficients

\displaystyle U_{ab(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{(4T_{a}-11T_{b})m_{a}m_{b}-2T_{a}m_{b}^{2}-5T_{b}m_{a}^{2}}{2(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})(m_{b}+m_{a})};

\displaystyle D_{ab(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,\frac{6T_{a}^{2}m_{a}m_{b}^{2}+2T_{a}^{2}m_{b}^{3}+21T_{a}T_{b}m_{a}^{2}m_{b}-5T_{a}T_{b}m_{a}m_{b}^{2}-30T_{b}^{2}m_{a}^{3}-52T_{b}^{2}m_{a}^{2}m_{b}}{10(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})^{2}(m_{b}+m_{a})};

\displaystyle D_{ab(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{3m_{b}T_{a}[(10T_{a}-11T_{b})m_{a}m_{b}+4T_{a}m_{b}^{2}-5T_{b}m_{a}^{2}]}{10(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})^{2}(m_{b}+m_{a})};

\displaystyle E_{ab(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,\frac{3T_{a}m_{b}[6T_{a}^{2}m_{a}m_{b}^{2}+2T_{a}^{2}m_{b}^{3}+27T_{a}T_{b}m_{a}^{2}m_{b}-11T_{a}T_{b}m_{a}m_{b}^{2}-84T_{b}^{2}m_{a}^{3}-118T_{b}^{2}m_{a}^{2}m_{b}]}{560(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})^{3}(m_{b}+m_{a})};

\displaystyle E_{ab(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,\frac{3m_{a}m_{b}T_{a}T_{b}[16T_{a}m_{a}m_{b}+10T_{a}m_{b}^{2}-5T_{b}m_{a}^{2}-11T_{b}m_{a}m_{b}]}{112(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})^{3}(m_{b}+m_{a})}.

As a double-check, we have verified that neglecting the 5th-order Hermite moments
\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{(5)}
in (
K44
) yields a model
that matches Burgers-Schunk; see equations (45)-(49) in
Schunk 1977
(after prescribing there Coulomb collisions).
For small temperature differences the mass-ratio coefficients simplify into

\displaystyle U_{ab(1)}=-\,\frac{(5/2)m_{a}+m_{b}}{m_{a}+m_{b}};

\displaystyle D_{ab(1)}=\frac{3m_{a}^{2}+\frac{1}{10}m_{a}m_{b}-\frac{1}{5}m_{b}^{2}}{(m_{a}+m_{b})^{2}};\qquad D_{ab(2)}=\frac{\frac{6}{5}m_{b}^{2}-\frac{3}{2}m_{a}m_{b}}{(m_{a}+m_{b})^{2}};

\displaystyle E_{ab(1)}=\frac{3}{560}\frac{m_{b}(84m_{a}^{2}+7m_{a}m_{b}-2m_{b}^{2})}{(m_{a}+m_{b})^{3}};\qquad E_{ab(2)}=\frac{15}{112}\frac{m_{a}m_{b}(m_{a}-2m_{b})}{(m_{a}+m_{b})^{3}}.

(K46)

The model is easily changed from Hermite moments to fluid moments by

\displaystyle\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}_{a}=\frac{p_{a}}{2}\sqrt{\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{a(3)};\qquad p_{a}\sqrt{\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{a(5)}=\frac{m_{a}}{T_{a}}\vec{\boldsymbol{X}}^{(5)}_{a}-28\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}_{a};

\displaystyle\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}_{b}=\frac{p_{b}}{2}\sqrt{\frac{T_{b}}{m_{b}}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)};\qquad p_{b}\sqrt{\frac{T_{b}}{m_{b}}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)}=\frac{m_{b}}{T_{b}}\vec{\boldsymbol{X}}^{(5)}_{b}-28\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}_{b}.

(K47)

The heat flux exchange rates become

\displaystyle\vec{\boldsymbol{Q}}^{(3)}_{ab}\,{}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\delta\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}_{ab}\,^{\prime}}{\delta t}=\frac{1}{2}\textrm{Tr}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}}_{ab}^{(3)}-\frac{5}{2}\frac{p_{a}}{\rho_{a}}\boldsymbol{R}_{ab}

(K48)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\nu_{ab}p_{a}(\boldsymbol{u}_{b}-\boldsymbol{u}_{a})\hat{U}_{ab(1)}-\nu_{ab}\hat{D}_{ab(1)}\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}_{a}+\nu_{ab}\hat{D}_{ab(2)}\frac{\rho_{a}}{\rho_{b}}\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}_{b}

\displaystyle+\nu_{ab}\hat{E}_{ab(1)}\frac{\rho_{a}}{p_{a}}\vec{\boldsymbol{X}}^{(5)}_{a}-\nu_{ab}\hat{E}_{ab(2)}\frac{\rho_{a}}{\rho_{b}}\frac{\rho_{b}}{p_{b}}\vec{\boldsymbol{X}}^{(5)}_{b},

with mass-ratio coefficients (introducing hat)

\displaystyle\hat{U}_{ab(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle U_{ab(1)}+\frac{5}{2};

\displaystyle\hat{D}_{ab(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle D_{ab(1)}+28E_{ab(1)}+\frac{3}{2}\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}\frac{\mu_{ab}}{T_{ab}}+\frac{15}{4}\frac{T_{a}^{2}}{m_{a}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{ab}^{2}}{T_{ab}^{2}};

\displaystyle\hat{D}_{ab(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle D_{ab(2)}-28E_{ab(2)}+\frac{3}{2}\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}\frac{\mu_{ab}}{T_{ab}}+\frac{15}{4}\frac{T_{a}T_{b}}{m_{a}m_{b}}\frac{\mu_{ab}^{2}}{T_{ab}^{2}};

\displaystyle\hat{E}_{ab(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle E_{ab(1)}+\frac{15}{112}\frac{T_{a}^{2}}{m_{a}^{2}}\frac{\mu_{ab}^{2}}{T_{ab}^{2}};

\displaystyle\hat{E}_{ab(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\Big(E_{ab(2)}-\frac{15}{112}\frac{T_{a}T_{b}}{m_{a}m_{b}}\frac{\mu_{ab}^{2}}{T_{ab}^{2}}\Big).

(K49)

Introducing summation over all “b” species and separating the self-collisions, the final results are given by
(
18
), (
19
).

K.6
5th-order moment exchange rates

We need to calculate collisional contributions for the right hand side of the evolution equation for vector
X^{a(5)}_{i}
,
which is obtained by calculating

\displaystyle\vec{\boldsymbol{Q}}^{(5)}_{ab}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\textrm{Tr}\textrm{Tr}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}}^{(5)}_{ab}=\frac{\delta\vec{\boldsymbol{X}}^{(5)}_{ab}}{\delta t}=m_{a}\int\boldsymbol{c}_{a}|\boldsymbol{c}_{a}|^{4}C_{ab}(f_{a})d^{3}v

(K50)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle m_{a}\int f_{a}\Big(\boldsymbol{A}^{ab}|\boldsymbol{c}_{a}|^{4}+4(\boldsymbol{A}^{ab}\cdot\boldsymbol{c}_{a})|\boldsymbol{c}_{a}|^{2}\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\Big)d^{3}v

\displaystyle+m_{a}\int f_{a}\Big(4(\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}^{ab}\cdot\boldsymbol{c}_{a})|\boldsymbol{c}_{a}|^{2}+4(\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}^{ab}:\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\boldsymbol{c}_{a})\boldsymbol{c}_{a}+2(\textrm{Tr}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}^{ab})|\boldsymbol{c}_{a}|^{2}\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\Big)d^{3}v.

Again, before integration of (
K50
) it is useful to apply the semi-linear approximation, which calculates step by step

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{A}^{ab}|\boldsymbol{c}_{a}|^{4}

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle 2\frac{c_{ab}}{m_{a}^{2}}\big(1+\frac{m_{a}}{m_{b}}\big)\frac{n_{b}m_{b}}{T_{b}}\Big(\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}\Big)^{2}\bigg[

(K51)

\displaystyle+\alpha\Big(\alpha^{3}\tilde{y}^{4}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}+4\alpha^{2}\tilde{y}^{2}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}})\Big)\Big(\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{\tilde{y}^{2}}-\frac{\textrm{erf}(\tilde{y}/\sqrt{2})}{\tilde{y}^{3}}\Big)

\displaystyle-\,\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{10}\alpha^{4}\tilde{y}^{4}\Big(\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)}-\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)})+(\tilde{y}^{2}-5)\frac{\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)}}{28}-(\tilde{y}^{2}-7)\frac{\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)})}{28}\Big)\bigg];

\displaystyle(\boldsymbol{A}^{ab}\cdot\boldsymbol{c}_{a})\boldsymbol{c}_{a}|\boldsymbol{c}_{a}|^{2}

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle 2\frac{c_{ab}}{m_{a}^{2}}\big(1+\frac{m_{a}}{m_{b}}\big)\frac{n_{b}m_{b}}{T_{b}}\Big(\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}\Big)^{2}\bigg[

(K52)

\displaystyle+\alpha\Big(\alpha^{3}\tilde{y}^{4}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}+\alpha^{2}\tilde{y}^{4}\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}}+3\alpha^{2}\tilde{y}^{2}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}})\Big)\Big(\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{\tilde{y}^{2}}-\frac{\textrm{erf}(\tilde{y}/\sqrt{2})}{\tilde{y}^{3}}\Big)

\displaystyle-\,\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{10}\alpha^{4}\tilde{y}^{2}\Big(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)})(1-\tilde{y}^{2})+\frac{\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)})}{28}(8\tilde{y}^{2}-\tilde{y}^{4}-5)\Big)\bigg];

\displaystyle\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}^{ab}:\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\boldsymbol{c}_{a}

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle 2\frac{c_{ab}}{m_{a}^{2}}n_{b}\sqrt{\frac{m_{b}}{T_{b}}}\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}\bigg\{\Big(\alpha^{2}\tilde{y}^{2}+2\alpha(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}})\Big)A_{1}^{\prime\prime}

(K53)

\displaystyle+\alpha^{2}\tilde{y}^{2}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)})\Big(\tilde{A}_{3}^{\prime\prime}+2\frac{\tilde{A}_{3}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}\Big)+\alpha^{2}\tilde{y}^{2}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)})\Big(\tilde{A}_{5}^{\prime\prime}+2\frac{\tilde{A}_{5}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}\Big)\bigg\};

\displaystyle\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}^{ab}\cdot\boldsymbol{c}_{a}|\boldsymbol{c}_{a}|^{2}

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle 2\frac{c_{ab}}{m_{a}^{2}}\frac{n_{b}}{\alpha}\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}\bigg\{\alpha^{3}\tilde{y}^{2}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime\prime}+\alpha^{2}\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}}\tilde{y}\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime}+\alpha^{2}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}})\Big(3\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}\Big)

(K54)

\displaystyle+\alpha^{3}\tilde{y}^{2}\Big[\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)}\tilde{y}\tilde{A}_{3}^{\prime}+\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)})\Big(\frac{\tilde{A}_{3}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}+\tilde{A}_{3}^{\prime\prime}\Big)\Big]

\displaystyle+\alpha^{3}\tilde{y}^{2}\Big[\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)}\tilde{y}\tilde{A}_{5}^{\prime}+\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)})\Big(\frac{\tilde{A}_{5}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}+\tilde{A}_{5}^{\prime\prime}\Big)\Big]\bigg\};

\displaystyle\big(\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}^{ab}:\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\big)\boldsymbol{c}_{a}

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle 2\frac{c_{ab}}{m_{a}^{2}}\frac{n_{b}}{\alpha}\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}\bigg\{\Big(\alpha^{3}\tilde{y}^{2}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}+\alpha^{2}\tilde{y}^{2}\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}}+2\alpha^{2}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}})\Big)A_{1}^{\prime\prime}

(K55)

\displaystyle+\alpha^{3}\tilde{y}^{2}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)})\Big(2\frac{\tilde{A}_{3}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}+\tilde{A}_{3}^{\prime\prime}\Big)+\alpha^{3}\tilde{y}^{2}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)})\Big(2\frac{\tilde{A}_{5}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}+\tilde{A}_{5}^{\prime\prime}\Big)\bigg\};

\displaystyle 2(\textrm{Tr}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}^{ab})\boldsymbol{c}_{a}|\boldsymbol{c}_{a}|^{2}

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle 2\frac{c_{ab}}{m_{a}^{2}}\frac{n_{b}}{\alpha}\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}\bigg\{\Big(\alpha^{3}\tilde{y}^{2}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}+\alpha^{2}\tilde{y}^{2}\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}}+2\alpha^{2}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}})\Big)\Big(4\frac{\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}+2\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime\prime}\Big)

(K56)

\displaystyle+\alpha^{3}\tilde{y}^{2}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)})\Big(8\frac{\tilde{A}_{3}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}+2\tilde{A}_{3}^{\prime\prime}\Big)+\alpha^{3}\tilde{y}^{2}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)})\Big(8\frac{\tilde{A}_{5}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}+2\tilde{A}_{5}^{\prime\prime}\Big)\bigg\}.

Collecting results together, the first part of (
K50
) becomes

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{A}^{ab}|\boldsymbol{c}_{a}|^{4}+4(\boldsymbol{A}^{ab}\cdot\boldsymbol{c}_{a})\boldsymbol{c}_{a}|\boldsymbol{c}_{a}|^{2}\simeq 2\frac{c_{ab}}{m_{a}^{2}}\big(1+\frac{m_{a}}{m_{b}}\big)\frac{n_{b}}{\alpha^{2}}\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}\bigg\{

\displaystyle+\alpha\Big(5\alpha^{3}\tilde{y}^{4}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}+4\alpha^{2}\tilde{y}^{4}\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}}+16\alpha^{2}\tilde{y}^{2}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}})\Big)\Big(\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{\tilde{y}^{2}}-\frac{\textrm{erf}(\tilde{y}/\sqrt{2})}{\tilde{y}^{3}}\Big)

\displaystyle-\,\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{10}\alpha^{4}\tilde{y}^{2}\Big(\tilde{y}^{2}\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)}+\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)})(4-5\tilde{y}^{2})+\tilde{y}^{2}(\tilde{y}^{2}-5)\frac{\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)}}{28}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)})}{28}(39\tilde{y}^{2}-5\tilde{y}^{4}-20)\Big)\bigg\},

(K57)

and the second part of (
K50
) reads

\displaystyle 4(\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}^{ab}\cdot\boldsymbol{c}_{a})|\boldsymbol{c}_{a}|^{2}+4(\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}^{ab}:\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\boldsymbol{c}_{a})\boldsymbol{c}_{a}+2(\textrm{Tr}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}^{ab})|\boldsymbol{c}_{a}|^{2}\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\simeq 2\frac{c_{ab}}{m_{a}^{2}}\frac{n_{b}}{\alpha}\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}\bigg\{

\displaystyle\qquad\alpha^{3}\tilde{y}^{2}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\Big(4\frac{\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}+10\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime\prime}\Big)+\alpha^{2}\tilde{y}^{2}\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}}\Big(8\frac{\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}+6\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime\prime}\Big)+\alpha^{2}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}})\Big(4\frac{\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}+24\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime\prime}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad+4\alpha^{3}\tilde{y}^{3}\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)}\tilde{A}_{3}^{\prime}+\alpha^{3}\tilde{y}^{2}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)})\Big(20\frac{\tilde{A}_{3}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}+10\tilde{A}_{3}^{\prime\prime}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad+4\alpha^{3}\tilde{y}^{3}\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)}\tilde{A}_{5}^{\prime}+\alpha^{3}\tilde{y}^{2}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)})\Big(20\frac{\tilde{A}_{5}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}+10\tilde{A}_{5}^{\prime\prime}\Big)\bigg\}.

(K58)

Now (
K50
) can be integrated, and the entire collisional integral can be written in a symbolic form

\displaystyle\vec{\boldsymbol{Q}}^{(5)}_{ab}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle m_{a}n_{a}3\alpha^{3}\nu_{ab}\sqrt{\frac{\pi}{2}}\big(1+\alpha^{2}\big)^{3/2}\Big(\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}\Big)^{5/2}\times

(K59)

\displaystyle\int\frac{e^{-\alpha^{2}\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{(2\pi)^{3/2}}\big(1-\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}}+\chi_{a}\big)\Big[\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}}\{\ref{eq:Thierry15}\}+\frac{1}{(1+\frac{m_{a}}{m_{b}})\alpha}\{\ref{eq:Thierry16}\}\Big]d^{3}\tilde{y},

where {
K57
} & {
K58
} represent only parts of the corresponding equations that are inside of curly brackets.
The integration yields

\displaystyle\vec{\boldsymbol{Q}}^{(5)}_{ab}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\textrm{Tr}\textrm{Tr}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}}_{ab}^{(5)}=\frac{\delta\vec{\boldsymbol{X}}^{(5)}_{ab}}{\delta t}=\nu_{ab}\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}\Big\{+p_{a}(\boldsymbol{u}_{b}-\boldsymbol{u}_{a})U_{ab(2)}

(K60)

\displaystyle-F_{ab(1)}\frac{p_{a}}{2}\sqrt{\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{a(3)}-F_{ab(2)}\frac{\rho_{a}}{\rho_{b}}\frac{p_{b}}{2}\sqrt{\frac{T_{b}}{m_{b}}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(3)}

\displaystyle-G_{ab(1)}p_{a}\sqrt{\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{a(5)}+G_{ab(2)}\frac{\rho_{a}}{\rho_{b}}p_{b}\sqrt{\frac{T_{b}}{m_{b}}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{h}}^{b(5)}\Big\},

with mass-ratio coefficients

\displaystyle U_{ab(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,\frac{16T_{a}^{2}m_{a}m_{b}^{2}-8T_{a}^{2}m_{b}^{3}+56T_{a}T_{b}m_{a}^{2}m_{b}-52T_{a}T_{b}m_{a}m_{b}^{2}-35T_{b}^{2}m_{a}^{3}-119T_{b}^{2}m_{a}^{2}m_{b}}{(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})^{2}(m_{b}+m_{a})};

\displaystyle F_{ab(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\big\{40T_{a}^{4}m_{a}m_{b}^{3}+8T_{a}^{4}m_{b}^{4}+180T_{a}^{3}T_{b}m_{a}^{2}m_{b}^{2}+68T_{a}^{3}T_{b}m_{a}m_{b}^{3}+315T_{a}^{2}T_{b}^{2}m_{a}^{3}m_{b}+207T_{a}^{2}T_{b}^{2}m_{a}^{2}m_{b}^{2}

\displaystyle+700T_{a}T_{b}^{3}m_{a}^{4}+392T_{a}T_{b}^{3}m_{a}^{3}m_{b}-280T_{b}^{4}m_{a}^{4}\big\}\big[5(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})^{3}(m_{b}+m_{a})T_{a}\big]^{-1};

\displaystyle F_{ab(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,\frac{3T_{a}m_{b}\big[16T_{a}^{2}m_{b}^{3}+140T_{a}T_{b}m_{a}^{2}m_{b}+72T_{a}T_{b}m_{a}m_{b}^{2}-35T_{b}^{2}m_{a}^{3}-119T_{b}^{2}m_{a}^{2}m_{b}\big]}{5(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})^{3}(m_{b}+m_{a})};

\displaystyle G_{ab(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,\big\{40T_{a}^{4}m_{a}m_{b}^{4}+8T_{a}^{4}m_{b}^{5}+220T_{a}^{3}T_{b}m_{a}^{2}m_{b}^{3}+140T_{a}^{3}T_{b}m_{a}m_{b}^{4}+495T_{a}^{2}T_{b}^{2}m_{a}^{3}m_{b}^{2}

\displaystyle+627T_{a}^{2}T_{b}^{2}m_{a}^{2}m_{b}^{3}+3640T_{a}T_{b}^{3}m_{a}^{4}m_{b}+1916T_{a}T_{b}^{3}m_{a}^{3}m_{b}^{2}-1400T_{b}^{4}m_{a}^{5}

\displaystyle-3304T_{b}^{4}m_{a}^{4}m_{b}\big\}\big[280(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})^{4}(m_{a}+m_{b})\big]^{-1};

\displaystyle G_{ab(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{3T_{a}T_{b}m_{a}^{2}m_{b}\big[8T_{a}^{2}m_{b}^{2}-32T_{a}T_{b}m_{a}m_{b}-28T_{a}T_{b}m_{b}^{2}+5T_{b}^{2}m_{a}^{2}+17T_{b}^{2}m_{a}m_{b}\big]}{8(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})^{4}(m_{a}+m_{b})}.

For small temperature differences the mass-ratio coefficients simplify into

\displaystyle U_{ab(2)}=\frac{35m_{a}^{2}+28m_{a}m_{b}+8m_{b}^{2}}{(m_{a}+m_{b})^{2}};

\displaystyle F_{ab(1)}=\frac{420m_{a}^{3}+287m_{a}^{2}m_{b}+100m_{a}m_{b}^{2}+8m_{b}^{3}}{5(m_{a}+m_{b})^{3}};

\displaystyle F_{ab(2)}=\frac{3}{5}\frac{m_{b}(35m_{a}^{2}-56m_{a}m_{b}-16m_{b}^{2})}{(m_{a}+m_{b})^{3}};

\displaystyle G_{ab(1)}=\frac{1400m_{a}^{4}-1736m_{a}^{3}m_{b}-675m_{a}^{2}m_{b}^{2}-172m_{a}m_{b}^{3}-8m_{b}^{4}}{280(m_{a}+m_{b})^{4}};

\displaystyle G_{ab(2)}=\frac{15}{8}\frac{m_{a}^{2}m_{b}(m_{a}-4m_{b})}{(m_{a}+m_{b})^{4}}.

(K62)

Rewritten with fluid moments, the exchange rates for the 5th-order moment become

\displaystyle\vec{\boldsymbol{Q}}^{(5)}_{ab}\,{}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\vec{\boldsymbol{Q}}^{(5)}_{ab}-35\frac{p_{a}^{2}}{\rho_{a}^{2}}\boldsymbol{R}_{ab}=\frac{\delta\vec{\boldsymbol{X}}^{(5)}_{ab}\,{}^{\prime}}{\delta t}=\nu_{ab}\Big\{-\frac{p_{a}^{2}}{\rho_{a}}(\boldsymbol{u}_{b}-\boldsymbol{u}_{a})\hat{U}_{ab(2)}

(K63)

\displaystyle-\hat{F}_{ab(1)}\frac{p_{a}}{\rho_{a}}\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}_{a}+\hat{F}_{ab(2)}\frac{p_{a}}{\rho_{a}}\frac{\rho_{a}}{\rho_{b}}\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}_{b}-\hat{G}_{ab(1)}\vec{\boldsymbol{X}}^{(5)}_{a}-\hat{G}_{ab(2)}\frac{p_{a}}{p_{b}}\vec{\boldsymbol{X}}^{(5)}_{b}\Big\},

with mass-ratio coefficients (introducing hat)

\displaystyle\hat{U}_{ab(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\big(U_{ab(2)}-35\big);

\displaystyle\hat{F}_{ab(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle F_{ab(1)}-28G_{ab(1)}+35\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}\frac{\mu_{ab}}{T_{ab}}V_{ab(1)};

\displaystyle\hat{F}_{ab(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\Big(F_{ab(2)}+28G_{ab(2)}-35\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}\frac{\mu_{ab}}{T_{ab}}V_{ab(2)}\Big);

\displaystyle\hat{G}_{ab(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\hat{G}_{ab(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\Big(G_{ab(2)}-\frac{15}{8}\frac{T_{a}T_{b}}{m_{a}m_{b}}\frac{\mu_{ab}^{2}}{T_{ab}^{2}}\Big).

Appendix L
Braginskii viscosity (15-moment model)

We use polynomials derived from the
reducible
Hermite polymials (see details in Appendix
B
),
with perturbation of the distribution function
f_{b}(\boldsymbol{v}^{\prime})=f_{b}^{(0)}(1+\chi_{b})

\chi_{b}=\frac{1}{2}\tilde{h}^{b(2)}_{ij}\tilde{H}^{b(2)}_{ij}+\frac{1}{28}\hat{h}^{b(4)}_{ij}\hat{H}^{b(4)}_{ij}.

(L1)

For clarity of calculations, we here only consider the viscous part of
\chi_{b}
(i.e. the 15-moment model) but the full 22-moment model
can be implicitly assumed for the final
collisional contributions at the semi-linear level. The Hermite polynomials are (dropping species index “b” for polynomials and velocities
\tilde{\boldsymbol{c}}
)

\displaystyle\tilde{H}^{(2)}_{ij}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tilde{c}_{i}\tilde{c}_{j}-\delta_{ij};

\displaystyle\tilde{H}^{(4)}_{ij}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\tilde{H}^{(4)}_{ijkk}=\big(\tilde{c}_{i}\tilde{c}_{j}-\frac{\delta_{ij}}{3}\tilde{c}^{2}\big)(\tilde{c}^{2}-7)+\frac{\delta_{ij}}{3}\tilde{H}^{(4)};

\displaystyle\tilde{H}^{(4)}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\tilde{H}^{(4)}_{iikk}=\tilde{c}^{4}-10\tilde{c}^{2}+15;

\displaystyle\hat{H}^{(4)}_{ij}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\tilde{H}^{(4)}_{ij}-\frac{\delta_{ij}}{3}\tilde{H}^{(4)}=\big(\tilde{c}_{i}\tilde{c}_{j}-\frac{\delta_{ij}}{3}\tilde{c}^{2}\big)(\tilde{c}^{2}-7).

(L2)

The irreducible polynomials yield the same perturbation
\chi_{b}
.
By using the perturbation (
L1
) one can calculate fluid moments
\Pi_{ij}^{b(2)}
,
\Pi_{ij}^{b(4)}
,
or one can directly calculate Hermite moments

\displaystyle\tilde{h}_{ij}^{b(2)}=\frac{1}{p_{b}}\Pi_{ij}^{b(2)};\qquad\hat{h}_{ij}^{b(4)}=\frac{\rho_{b}}{p_{b}^{2}}\Pi_{ij}^{b(4)}-\frac{7}{p_{b}}\Pi_{ij}^{b(2)},

(L3)

yielding the same relations. Both
\tilde{h}_{ij}^{(2)}
and
\hat{h}_{ij}^{(4)}
are traceless (and
\tilde{h}_{ij}^{(2)}=\hat{h}_{ij}^{(2)}
).

L.1
Rosenbluth potentials

The notation reads

\tilde{\boldsymbol{c}}_{b}=\sqrt{\frac{m_{b}}{T_{b}}}(\boldsymbol{v}^{\prime}-\boldsymbol{u}_{b});\qquad|\boldsymbol{v}^{\prime}-\boldsymbol{v}|=\sqrt{\frac{T_{b}}{m_{b}}}|\tilde{\boldsymbol{c}}_{b}-\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}|;\qquad\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}=\sqrt{\frac{m_{b}}{T_{b}}}(\boldsymbol{v}-\boldsymbol{u}_{b}),

the Rosenbluth potentials are

\displaystyle H_{b}(\boldsymbol{v})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int\frac{f_{b}(\boldsymbol{v}^{\prime})}{|\boldsymbol{v}^{\prime}-\boldsymbol{v}|}d^{3}v^{\prime}=n_{b}\sqrt{\frac{m_{b}}{T_{b}}}\int\frac{\phi^{(0)}_{b}}{|\tilde{\boldsymbol{c}}_{b}-\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}|}(1+\chi_{b})d^{3}\tilde{c}_{b};

\displaystyle G_{b}(\boldsymbol{v})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int|\boldsymbol{v}^{\prime}-\boldsymbol{v}|f_{b}(\boldsymbol{v}^{\prime})d^{3}v^{\prime}=n_{b}\sqrt{\frac{T_{b}}{m_{b}}}\int|\tilde{\boldsymbol{c}}_{b}-\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}|\phi^{(0)}_{b}(1+\chi_{b})d^{3}\tilde{c}_{b},

and further calculate

\displaystyle H_{b}(\boldsymbol{v})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{b}\sqrt{\frac{m_{b}}{T_{b}}}\Big\{\frac{1}{\tilde{y}}\textrm{erf}\Big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\Big)+\frac{1}{2}(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\Big[\frac{3}{\tilde{y}^{5}}\textrm{erf}\Big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\Big)-\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\big(\frac{1}{\tilde{y}^{2}}+\frac{3}{\tilde{y}^{4}}\big)e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}\Big]

(L4)

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{1}{28}(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}\Big\};

\displaystyle G_{b}(\boldsymbol{v})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{b}\sqrt{\frac{T_{b}}{m_{b}}}\Big\{\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}+\big(\tilde{y}+\frac{1}{\tilde{y}}\big)\textrm{erf}\big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\big)

(L5)

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{1}{2}(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\Big[\frac{3}{\tilde{y}^{4}}\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}+\big(\frac{1}{\tilde{y}^{3}}-\frac{3}{\tilde{y}^{5}}\big)\textrm{erf}\Big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\Big)\Big]

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{1}{14}(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\Big[\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\big(\frac{1}{\tilde{y}^{2}}+\frac{3}{\tilde{y}^{4}}\big)e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}-\frac{3}{\tilde{y}^{5}}\textrm{erf}\Big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\Big)\Big]\Big\}.

The derivative of the first Rosenbluth potential becomes

\displaystyle\frac{\partial H_{b}}{\partial\boldsymbol{v}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{n_{b}m_{b}}{T_{b}}\Big\{\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\Big(\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{\tilde{y}^{2}}-\frac{\textrm{erf}(\tilde{y}/\sqrt{2})}{\tilde{y}^{3}}\Big)

(L6)

\displaystyle+(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\Big[\frac{3}{\tilde{y}^{5}}\textrm{erf}\Big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\Big)-\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\big(\frac{1}{\tilde{y}^{2}}+\frac{3}{\tilde{y}^{4}}\big)e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\Big[-\frac{15}{\tilde{y}^{7}}\textrm{erf}\Big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\Big)+\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\big(\frac{1}{\tilde{y}^{2}}+\frac{5}{\tilde{y}^{4}}+\frac{15}{\tilde{y}^{6}}\big)e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{14}(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}+\frac{1}{28}(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}\Big\}.

For the second Rosenbluth potential it is useful to use a form

\displaystyle G_{b}(\boldsymbol{v})=n_{b}\sqrt{\frac{T_{b}}{m_{b}}}\Big[\tilde{A}_{1}+\tilde{A}_{2}(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})+\tilde{A}_{4}(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\Big],

(L7)

where

\displaystyle\tilde{A}_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}+\big(\tilde{y}+\frac{1}{\tilde{y}}\big)\textrm{erf}\big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\big);

\displaystyle\tilde{A}_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\Big[\frac{3}{\tilde{y}^{4}}\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}+\big(\frac{1}{\tilde{y}^{3}}-\frac{3}{\tilde{y}^{5}}\big)\textrm{erf}\Big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\Big)\Big];

\displaystyle\tilde{A}_{4}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{14}\Big[\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\big(\frac{1}{\tilde{y}^{2}}+\frac{3}{\tilde{y}^{4}}\big)e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}-\frac{3}{\tilde{y}^{5}}\textrm{erf}\Big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\Big)\Big].

(L8)

Its second derivative then calculates

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial\boldsymbol{v}}\frac{\partial G_{b}}{\partial\boldsymbol{v}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{b}\sqrt{\frac{m_{b}}{T_{b}}}\Big\{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}\frac{\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}+\frac{\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}}{\tilde{y}^{2}}\Big(\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}\Big)

(L9)

\displaystyle+\Big[2\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})+2(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}+\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\Big]\frac{\tilde{A}_{2}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}

\displaystyle+2\tilde{A}_{2}\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}+\frac{\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}}{\tilde{y}^{2}}(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\Big(\tilde{A}_{2}^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\tilde{A}_{2}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big[2\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})+2(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}+\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\Big]\frac{\tilde{A}_{4}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}

\displaystyle+2\tilde{A}_{4}\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}+\frac{\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}}{\tilde{y}^{2}}(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\Big(\tilde{A}_{4}^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\tilde{A}_{4}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}\Big)\Big\},

with coefficients

\displaystyle\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{\tilde{y}}+\big(1-\frac{1}{\tilde{y}^{2}}\big)\textrm{erf}\big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\big);

\displaystyle\tilde{A}_{2}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\big(\frac{1}{\tilde{y}^{3}}+\frac{15}{2\tilde{y}^{5}}\big)\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}+\frac{3}{2}\big(\frac{1}{\tilde{y}^{4}}-\frac{{5}}{\tilde{y}^{6}}\big)\textrm{erf}\big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\big);

\displaystyle\tilde{A}_{4}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{14}\big(\frac{1}{\tilde{y}}+\frac{5}{\tilde{y}^{3}}+\frac{15}{\tilde{y}^{5}}\big)\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}-\frac{15}{14\tilde{y}^{6}}\textrm{erf}\big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\big);

\displaystyle\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,\frac{2}{\tilde{y}^{2}}\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}+\frac{2}{\tilde{y}^{3}}\textrm{erf}\big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\big);

\displaystyle\tilde{A}_{2}^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\big(\frac{1}{\tilde{y}^{2}}+\frac{9}{\tilde{y}^{4}}+\frac{45}{\tilde{y}^{6}}\big)\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}+\big(-\frac{6}{\tilde{y}^{5}}+\frac{45}{\tilde{y}^{7}}\big)\textrm{erf}\big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\big);

\displaystyle\tilde{A}_{4}^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,\frac{1}{14}\big(1+\frac{6}{\tilde{y}^{2}}+\frac{30}{\tilde{y}^{4}}+\frac{90}{\tilde{y}^{6}}\big)\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}+\frac{45}{7\tilde{y}^{7}}\textrm{erf}\big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\big),

(L10)

and

\displaystyle\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,\frac{3}{\tilde{y}^{2}}\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}-\big(\frac{1}{\tilde{y}}-\frac{3}{\tilde{y}^{3}}\big)\textrm{erf}\big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\big);

\displaystyle\tilde{A}_{2}^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\tilde{A}_{2}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\big(\frac{1}{\tilde{y}^{2}}+\frac{10}{\tilde{y}^{4}}+\frac{105}{2\tilde{y}^{6}}\big)\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}-\frac{15}{2}\big(\frac{1}{\tilde{y}^{5}}-\frac{7}{\tilde{y}^{7}}\big)\textrm{erf}\big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\big);

\displaystyle\tilde{A}_{4}^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\tilde{A}_{4}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,\frac{1}{14}\big(1+\frac{7}{\tilde{y}^{2}}+\frac{35}{\tilde{y}^{4}}+\frac{105}{\tilde{y}^{6}}\big)\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}+\frac{15}{2\tilde{y}^{7}}\textrm{erf}\big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\big).

(L11)

As a double-check, applying
(1/2)\textrm{Tr}
on (
L9
) yields

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\textrm{Tr}\frac{\partial}{\partial\boldsymbol{v}}\frac{\partial G_{b}}{\partial\boldsymbol{v}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{b}\sqrt{\frac{m_{b}}{T_{b}}}\frac{1}{2}\Big\{\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime\prime}+2\frac{\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}+(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\big(\tilde{A}_{2}^{\prime\prime}+6\frac{\tilde{A}_{2}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}\big)

(L12)

\displaystyle+(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\big(\tilde{A}_{4}^{\prime\prime}+6\frac{\tilde{A}_{4}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}\big)\Big\}=H_{b},

recovering the first Rosenbluth potential (
L4
). Similarly, applying
(\partial/\partial\boldsymbol{v})\cdot
on (
L6
) recovers

-4\pi f_{b}(\boldsymbol{v})
. Both Rosenbluth potentials seem to be calculated correctly.

L.2
Dynamical friction vector and diffusion tensor

The dynamical friction vector becomes

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{A}^{ab}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\frac{c_{ab}}{m_{a}^{2}}\big(1+\frac{m_{a}}{m_{b}}\big)\frac{n_{b}m_{b}}{T_{b}}\Big\{\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\Big(\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{\tilde{y}^{2}}-\frac{\textrm{erf}(\tilde{y}/\sqrt{2})}{\tilde{y}^{3}}\Big)

(L13)

\displaystyle+(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\Big[\frac{3}{\tilde{y}^{5}}\textrm{erf}\Big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\Big)-\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\big(\frac{1}{\tilde{y}^{2}}+\frac{3}{\tilde{y}^{4}}\big)e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\Big[-\frac{15}{\tilde{y}^{7}}\textrm{erf}\Big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\Big)+\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\big(\frac{1}{\tilde{y}^{2}}+\frac{5}{\tilde{y}^{4}}+\frac{15}{\tilde{y}^{6}}\big)e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{14}(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}+\frac{1}{28}(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}\Big\},

and the diffusion tensor

\displaystyle\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}^{ab}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\frac{c_{ab}}{m_{a}^{2}}n_{b}\sqrt{\frac{m_{b}}{T_{b}}}\Big\{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}\frac{\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}+\frac{\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}}{\tilde{y}^{2}}\Big(\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}\Big)

(L14)

\displaystyle+\Big[2\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})+2(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}+\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\Big]\frac{\tilde{A}_{2}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}

\displaystyle+2\tilde{A}_{2}\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}+\frac{\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}}{\tilde{y}^{2}}(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\Big(\tilde{A}_{2}^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\tilde{A}_{2}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big[2\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})+2(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}+\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\Big]\frac{\tilde{A}_{4}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}

\displaystyle+2\tilde{A}_{4}\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}+\frac{\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}}{\tilde{y}^{2}}(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\Big(\tilde{A}_{4}^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\tilde{A}_{4}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}\Big)\Big\},

where
c_{ab}=2\pi e^{4}Z_{a}^{2}Z_{b}^{2}\ln\Lambda
.

L.3
Distribution function for species “a”

To avoid the complicated runaway effect, the distribution function
f_{a}(\boldsymbol{v})=f_{a}^{(0)}(1+\chi_{a})
has to be expanded for small drifts,
in the semi-linear approximation.
Following the derivation and notation introduced in Section
K.3
, the expanded distribution function becomes

f_{a}=n_{a}\Big(\frac{m_{a}}{T_{a}}\Big)^{3/2}\frac{e^{-\alpha^{2}\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{(2\pi)^{3/2}}(1-\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}}+\chi_{a}),

(L15)

now with perturbation

\chi_{a}=\frac{1}{2}\tilde{h}^{a(2)}_{ij}\tilde{H}^{a(2)}_{ij}(\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})+\frac{1}{28}\hat{h}^{a(4)}_{ij}\hat{H}^{a(4)}_{ij}(\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}),

(L16)

where

\displaystyle\tilde{h}^{a(2)}_{ij}\tilde{H}^{a(2)}_{ij}(\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})=\tilde{h}^{a(2)}_{ij}\alpha^{2}\tilde{y}_{i}\tilde{y}_{j};

\displaystyle\hat{h}^{a(4)}_{ij}\hat{H}^{a(4)}_{ij}(\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})=\hat{h}^{a(4)}_{ij}\alpha^{2}\tilde{y}_{i}\tilde{y}_{j}(\alpha^{2}\tilde{y}^{2}-7),

(L17)

so the perturbation reads

\chi_{a}=\frac{\alpha^{2}}{2}(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{a(2)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})+\frac{\alpha^{2}}{28}(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{a(4)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\big(\alpha^{2}\tilde{y}^{2}-7\big).

(L18)

As a reminder

\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}}=(\boldsymbol{u}_{b}-\boldsymbol{u}_{a})\sqrt{\frac{m_{a}}{T_{a}}};\qquad\alpha=\frac{\sqrt{T_{b}/m_{b}}}{\sqrt{T_{a}/m_{a}}}.

L.4
Pressure tensor exchange rates

We need to calculate collisional contributions for the r.h.s. of the pressure tensor equation, and these contributions read

\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}}_{ab}^{(2)}=m_{a}\int f_{a}\big[\boldsymbol{A}_{ab}\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\big]^{S}d^{3}v+m_{a}\int f_{a}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}_{ab}d^{3}v.

(L19)

By emplying

\boldsymbol{c}_{a}=\sqrt{\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}}(\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}+\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}}),

in the semi-linear approximation

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{A}^{ab}\boldsymbol{c}_{a}

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle 2\frac{c_{ab}}{m_{a}^{2}}\big(1+\frac{m_{a}}{m_{b}}\big)\frac{n_{b}m_{b}}{T_{b}}\sqrt{\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}}\Big\{\big(\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}+\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}}\big)\Big(\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{\tilde{y}^{2}}-\frac{\textrm{erf}(\tilde{y}/\sqrt{2})}{\tilde{y}^{3}}\Big)

(L20)

\displaystyle+\alpha(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\Big[\frac{3}{\tilde{y}^{5}}\textrm{erf}\Big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\Big)-\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\big(\frac{1}{\tilde{y}^{2}}+\frac{3}{\tilde{y}^{4}}\big)e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{\alpha}{2}(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\Big[-\frac{15}{\tilde{y}^{7}}\textrm{erf}\Big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\Big)+\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\big(\frac{1}{\tilde{y}^{2}}+\frac{5}{\tilde{y}^{4}}+\frac{15}{\tilde{y}^{6}}\big)e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{\alpha}{14}(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}+\frac{\alpha}{28}(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}\Big\},

and

\displaystyle\big[\boldsymbol{A}^{ab}\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\big]^{S}

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle 2\frac{c_{ab}}{m_{a}^{2}}\big(1+\frac{m_{a}}{m_{b}}\big)\frac{n_{b}m_{b}}{T_{b}}\sqrt{\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}}\Big\{\big(2\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}+\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}}+\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\big)\Big(\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{\tilde{y}^{2}}-\frac{\textrm{erf}(\tilde{y}/\sqrt{2})}{\tilde{y}^{3}}\Big)

(L21)

\displaystyle+\alpha\Big((\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}+\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\Big)\Big[\frac{3}{\tilde{y}^{5}}\textrm{erf}\Big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\Big)-\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\big(\frac{1}{\tilde{y}^{2}}+\frac{3}{\tilde{y}^{4}}\big)e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}\Big]

\displaystyle+\alpha(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\Big[-\frac{15}{\tilde{y}^{7}}\textrm{erf}\Big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\Big)+\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\big(\frac{1}{\tilde{y}^{2}}+\frac{5}{\tilde{y}^{4}}+\frac{15}{\tilde{y}^{6}}\big)e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{\alpha}{14}\Big((\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}+\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\Big)\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}+\frac{\alpha}{14}(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}\Big\}.

The first term of (
L19
) is rewritten as

\displaystyle m_{a}\int f_{a}\big[\boldsymbol{A}_{ab}\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\big]^{S}d^{3}v=m_{a}n_{a}\alpha^{3}\int\frac{e^{-\alpha^{2}\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{(2\pi)^{3/2}}(1-\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}}+\chi_{a})\big[\boldsymbol{A}_{ab}\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\big]^{S}d^{3}\tilde{y},

(L22)

and by using the following integrals

\displaystyle\int\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}f(\tilde{y})e^{-\alpha^{2}\tilde{y}^{2}/2}d^{3}y

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}\frac{4\pi}{3}\int_{0}^{\infty}\tilde{y}^{4}f(\tilde{y})e^{-\alpha^{2}\tilde{y}^{2}/2}d\tilde{y};

\displaystyle\int\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})f(\tilde{y})e^{-\alpha^{2}\tilde{y}^{2}/2}d^{3}\tilde{y}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}\frac{4\pi}{3}\int_{0}^{\infty}\tilde{y}^{4}f(\tilde{y})e^{-\alpha^{2}\tilde{y}^{2}/2}d\tilde{y};

\displaystyle\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}:\int\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}f(\tilde{y})e^{-\alpha^{2}\tilde{y}^{2}/2}d^{3}\tilde{y}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0;

\displaystyle\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}:\int\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}f(\tilde{y})e^{-\alpha^{2}\tilde{y}^{2}/2}d^{3}\tilde{y}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}\frac{8\pi}{15}\int_{0}^{\infty}\tilde{y}^{6}f(\tilde{y})e^{-\alpha^{2}\tilde{y}^{2}/2}d\tilde{y},

(L23)

and by further applying the semi-linear approximation it integrates

\displaystyle m_{a}\int f_{a}\big[\boldsymbol{A}_{ab}\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\big]^{S}d^{3}v

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\rho_{a}\nu_{ab}\Big[-2\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}+\frac{6}{5}\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}\frac{T_{a}T_{b}}{T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a}}-\frac{3}{7}\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}\frac{m_{a}T_{a}T_{b}^{2}}{(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})^{2}}

(L24)

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{5}\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{a(2)}\frac{2T_{a}m_{b}+5T_{b}m_{a}}{T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a}}+\frac{3}{35}\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{a(4)}\frac{T_{a}m_{b}(2T_{a}m_{b}+7T_{b}m_{a})}{(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})^{2}}\Big].

Similarly, the second term of (
L19
) integrates

\displaystyle m_{a}\int f_{a}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}_{ab}d^{3}v

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\rho_{a}\nu_{ab}}{m_{a}+m_{b}}\Big[\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}\frac{2}{m_{a}}(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})-\frac{2}{5}T_{b}\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}+\frac{3}{35}\frac{m_{a}T_{b}^{2}}{T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a}}\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}

(L25)

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{5}\frac{T_{a}m_{b}}{m_{a}}\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{a(2)}+\frac{3}{35}\frac{m_{b}^{2}T_{a}^{2}}{m_{a}(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})}\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{a(4)}\Big].

Adding the last two equations together finally yields

\displaystyle\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}}_{ab}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\rho_{a}\nu_{ab}}{m_{a}+m_{b}}\Big[+2(T_{b}-T_{a})\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}-K_{ab(1)}T_{a}\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{a(2)}+K_{ab(2)}T_{b}\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}

(L26)

\displaystyle+L_{ab(1)}T_{a}\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{a(4)}-L_{ab(2)}T_{b}\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}\Big],

with mass-ratio coefficients

\displaystyle K_{ab(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2(2T_{a}m_{a}m_{b}+3T_{a}m_{b}^{2}+5T_{b}m_{a}^{2}+6T_{b}m_{a}m_{b})}{5(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})m_{a}};

\displaystyle K_{ab(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2(3T_{a}m_{a}+2T_{a}m_{b}-T_{b}m_{a})}{5(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})};

\displaystyle L_{ab(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{3T_{a}m_{b}(2T_{a}m_{a}m_{b}+3T_{a}m_{b}^{2}+7T_{b}m_{a}^{2}+8T_{b}m_{a}m_{b})}{35(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})^{2}m_{a}};

\displaystyle L_{ab(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{3m_{a}T_{b}(5T_{a}m_{a}+4T_{a}m_{b}-T_{b}m_{a})}{35(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})^{2}}.

(L27)

As a partial double-check of the entire formulation, by neglecting the 4th-order Hermite moments
\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{(4)}
in (
L26
)
it can be verified that the model is then equivalent to Burgers-Schunk; see equation (44) in
Schunk 1977
, or
our previous equation (
J21
).
For a particular case of small temperature differences, the mass-ratio coefficients simplify into

\displaystyle K_{ab(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2(5m_{a}+3m_{b})}{5m_{a}};\qquad K_{ab(2)}=\frac{4}{5};

\displaystyle L_{ab(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{3(7m_{a}+3m_{b})m_{b}}{35m_{a}(m_{b}+m_{a})};\qquad L_{ab(2)}=\frac{12m_{a}}{35(m_{a}+m_{b})},

(L28)

and for self-collisions
K_{aa(1)}=16/5
;
K_{aa(2)}=4/5
;
L_{aa(1)}=3/7
;
L_{aa(2)}=6/35
.

L.5
Viscosity-tensor exhange rates

Collisional contributions for the viscosity-tensor
\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}^{(2)}_{a}
become

\displaystyle\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}}_{ab}^{(2)}\,{}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\delta\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}^{(2)}_{ab}}{\delta t}=\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}}_{ab}^{(2)}-\frac{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}}{3}\textrm{Tr}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}}_{ab}^{(2)}

(L29)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\rho_{a}\nu_{ab}}{m_{a}+m_{b}}\Big[-K_{ab(1)}T_{a}\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{a(2)}+K_{ab(2)}T_{b}\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}

\displaystyle+L_{ab(1)}T_{a}\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{a(4)}-L_{ab(2)}T_{b}\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}\Big],

and introducing summation over all “b” species and rewritten with fluid moments

\displaystyle\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}}_{a}^{(2)}\,{}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,\frac{21}{10}\nu_{aa}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}_{a}^{(2)}+\frac{9}{70}\nu_{aa}\frac{\rho_{a}}{p_{a}}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}_{a}^{(4)}

(L30)

\displaystyle+\sum_{b\neq a}\frac{\rho_{a}\nu_{ab}}{m_{a}+m_{b}}\Big[-\big(K_{ab(1)}+7L_{ab(1)}\big)\frac{1}{n_{a}}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}_{a}^{(2)}+\big(K_{ab(2)}+7L_{ab(2)}\big)\frac{1}{n_{b}}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}_{b}^{(2)}

\displaystyle+L_{ab(1)}\frac{\rho_{a}}{n_{a}p_{a}}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}_{a}^{(4)}-L_{ab(2)}\frac{\rho_{b}}{n_{b}p_{b}}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}_{b}^{(4)}\Big].

It is useful to define (introducing hat)

\hat{K}_{ab(1)}=K_{ab(1)}+7L_{ab(1)};\qquad\hat{K}_{ab(2)}=K_{ab(2)}+7L_{ab(2)},

(L31)

and the final mass-ratio coefficients are given by (
23
).

L.6
4th-order moment exchange rates

We need to calculate collisional contributions

\displaystyle\textrm{Tr}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}}_{ab}^{(4)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\delta\textrm{Tr}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{r}}}_{ab}}{\delta t}=m_{a}\int\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\boldsymbol{c}_{a}|\boldsymbol{c}_{a}|^{2}C_{ab}(f_{a})d^{3}v

(L32)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle m_{a}\int f_{a}\Big[(\boldsymbol{A}^{ab}\boldsymbol{c}_{a})^{S}|\boldsymbol{c}_{a}|^{2}+2(\boldsymbol{A}^{ab}\cdot\boldsymbol{c}_{a})\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\Big]d^{3}v

\displaystyle+m_{a}\int f_{a}\Big[(\textrm{Tr}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}^{ab})\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\boldsymbol{c}_{a}+\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}^{ab}|\boldsymbol{c}_{a}|^{2}+2\big((\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}^{ab}\cdot\boldsymbol{c}_{a})\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\big)^{S}\Big]d^{3}v.

There will be no
\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}}
contributions at the end, and it is simpler to supress these from the beginning (
\tilde{\boldsymbol{u}}=0
),
and just use
\boldsymbol{c}_{a}=\sqrt{T_{a}/m_{a}}\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}
. Then one evaluates step by step

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{A}^{ab}\cdot\boldsymbol{c}_{a}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\frac{c_{ab}}{m_{a}^{2}}\big(1+\frac{m_{a}}{m_{b}}\big)\frac{n_{b}m_{b}}{T_{b}}\sqrt{\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}}\alpha\Big\{\Big(\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}-\frac{\textrm{erf}(\tilde{y}/\sqrt{2})}{\tilde{y}}\Big)

(L33)

\displaystyle+(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\Big[-\,\frac{9}{2\tilde{y}^{5}}\textrm{erf}\Big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\Big)+\frac{1}{2}\big(1+\frac{3}{\tilde{y}^{2}}+\frac{9}{\tilde{y}^{4}}\big)\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{28}(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\big(-2+\tilde{y}^{2}\big)\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}\Big\};

\displaystyle 2\big(\boldsymbol{A}^{ab}\cdot\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\big)\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\boldsymbol{c}_{a}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\frac{c_{ab}}{m_{a}^{2}}\big(1+\frac{m_{a}}{m_{b}}\big)\frac{n_{b}m_{b}}{T_{b}}\sqrt{\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}}\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}\alpha^{3}\Big\{2\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\Big(\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}-\frac{\textrm{erf}(\tilde{y}/\sqrt{2})}{\tilde{y}}\Big)

(L34)

\displaystyle+(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\Big[-\,\frac{9}{\tilde{y}^{5}}\textrm{erf}\Big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\Big)+\big(1+\frac{3}{\tilde{y}^{2}}+\frac{9}{\tilde{y}^{4}}\big)\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{14}(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\big(-2+\tilde{y}^{2}\big)\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}\Big\};

\displaystyle\big(\boldsymbol{A}^{ab}\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\big)^{S}|\boldsymbol{c}_{a}|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\frac{c_{ab}}{m_{a}^{2}}\big(1+\frac{m_{a}}{m_{b}}\big)\frac{n_{b}m_{b}}{T_{b}}\sqrt{\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}}\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}\alpha^{3}\Big\{2\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\Big(\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}-\frac{\textrm{erf}(\tilde{y}/\sqrt{2})}{\tilde{y}}\Big)

(L35)

\displaystyle+\Big((\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}+\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\Big)\Big[\frac{3}{\tilde{y}^{3}}\textrm{erf}\Big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\Big)-\big(1+\frac{3}{\tilde{y}^{2}}\big)\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}\Big]

\displaystyle+(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\Big[-\frac{15}{\tilde{y}^{5}}\textrm{erf}\Big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\Big)+\big(1+\frac{5}{\tilde{y}^{2}}+\frac{15}{\tilde{y}^{4}}\big)\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{14}\Big((\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}+\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\Big)\tilde{y}^{2}\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}+\frac{1}{14}(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{y}^{2}\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}\Big\},

and adding the last two results together

\displaystyle\big(\boldsymbol{A}^{ab}\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\big)^{S}|\boldsymbol{c}_{a}|^{2}+2\big(\boldsymbol{A}^{ab}\cdot\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\big)\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\boldsymbol{c}_{a}

\displaystyle=2\frac{c_{ab}}{m_{a}^{2}}\big(1+\frac{m_{a}}{m_{b}}\big)\frac{n_{b}m_{b}}{T_{b}}\sqrt{\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}}\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}\alpha^{3}\Big\{4\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\Big(\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}-\frac{\textrm{erf}(\tilde{y}/\sqrt{2})}{\tilde{y}}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big((\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}+\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\Big)\Big[\frac{3}{\tilde{y}^{3}}\textrm{erf}\Big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\Big)-\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\big(1+\frac{3}{\tilde{y}^{2}}\big)e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}\Big]

\displaystyle+(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\Big[-\,\frac{24}{\tilde{y}^{5}}\textrm{erf}\Big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\Big)+\big(2+\frac{8}{\tilde{y}^{2}}+\frac{24}{\tilde{y}^{4}}\big)\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}\Big]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{14}\Big((\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}+\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\Big)\tilde{y}^{2}\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}+\frac{1}{14}(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\big(-2+2\tilde{y}^{2}\big)\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}\Big\}.

(L36)

Similarly for the diffusion tensor, calculating step by step

\displaystyle\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}^{ab}\cdot\boldsymbol{c}_{a}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\frac{c_{ab}}{m_{a}^{2}}n_{b}\sqrt{\frac{m_{b}}{T_{b}}}\sqrt{\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}}\alpha\Big\{\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime\prime}

(L37)

\displaystyle+(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\Big(2\tilde{y}\tilde{A}_{2}^{\prime}+2\tilde{A}_{2}\Big)+\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\Big(\tilde{A}_{2}^{\prime\prime}+2\frac{\tilde{A}_{2}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}\Big)

\displaystyle+(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\Big(2\tilde{y}\tilde{A}_{4}^{\prime}+2\tilde{A}_{4}\Big)+\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\Big(\tilde{A}_{4}^{\prime\prime}+2\frac{\tilde{A}_{4}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}\Big)\Big\};

\displaystyle\textrm{Tr}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}^{ab}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\frac{c_{ab}}{m_{a}^{2}}n_{b}\sqrt{\frac{m_{b}}{T_{b}}}\Big\{\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime\prime}+2\frac{\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}

(L38)

\displaystyle+(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\Big(\tilde{A}_{2}^{\prime\prime}+6\frac{\tilde{A}_{2}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}\Big)+(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\Big(\tilde{A}_{4}^{\prime\prime}+6\frac{\tilde{A}_{4}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}\Big)\Big\};

\displaystyle\textrm{Tr}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}^{ab}\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\boldsymbol{c}_{a}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\frac{c_{ab}}{m_{a}^{2}}n_{b}\sqrt{\frac{m_{b}}{T_{b}}}\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}\alpha^{2}\Big\{\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\Big(\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime\prime}+2\frac{\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}\Big)

(L39)

\displaystyle+(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\Big(\tilde{A}_{2}^{\prime\prime}+6\frac{\tilde{A}_{2}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}\Big)+(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\Big(\tilde{A}_{4}^{\prime\prime}+6\frac{\tilde{A}_{4}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}\Big)\Big\};

\displaystyle\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}^{ab}|\boldsymbol{c}_{a}|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\frac{c_{ab}}{m_{a}^{2}}n_{b}\sqrt{\frac{m_{b}}{T_{b}}}\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}\alpha^{2}\Big\{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}\tilde{y}\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime}+\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\Big(\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}\Big)

(L40)

\displaystyle+\Big[2\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})+2(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}+\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\Big]\tilde{y}\tilde{A}_{2}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+2\tilde{y}^{2}\tilde{A}_{2}\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}+\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\Big(\tilde{A}_{2}^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\tilde{A}_{2}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big[2\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})+2(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}+\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\Big]\tilde{y}\tilde{A}_{4}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+2\tilde{y}^{2}\tilde{A}_{4}\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}+\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\Big(\tilde{A}_{4}^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\tilde{A}_{4}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}\Big);

\displaystyle 2\big[(\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}^{ab}\cdot\boldsymbol{c}_{a})\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\big]^{S}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\frac{c_{ab}}{m_{a}^{2}}n_{b}\sqrt{\frac{m_{b}}{T_{b}}}\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}\alpha^{2}\Big\{4\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime\prime}

(L41)

\displaystyle+2\big[(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\big]^{S}\Big(2\tilde{y}\tilde{A}_{2}^{\prime}+2\tilde{A}_{2}\Big)+4\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\Big(\tilde{A}_{2}^{\prime\prime}+2\frac{\tilde{A}_{2}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}\Big)

\displaystyle+2\big[(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\big]^{S}\Big(2\tilde{y}\tilde{A}_{4}^{\prime}+2\tilde{A}_{4}\Big)+4\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\Big(\tilde{A}_{4}^{\prime\prime}+2\frac{\tilde{A}_{4}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}\Big)\Big\},

and adding the last three results together
