\displaystyle+2\big[(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\big]^{S}\Big(3\tilde{y}\tilde{A}_{2}^{\prime}+2\tilde{A}_{2}\Big)+\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\tilde{y}\tilde{A}_{2}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+2\tilde{y}^{2}\tilde{A}_{2}\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}+\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\Big(6\tilde{A}_{2}^{\prime\prime}+13\frac{\tilde{A}_{2}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}\Big)

\displaystyle+2\big[(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}\cdot\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\big]^{S}\Big(3\tilde{y}\tilde{A}_{4}^{\prime}+2\tilde{A}_{4}\Big)+\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\tilde{y}\tilde{A}_{4}^{\prime}

\displaystyle+2\tilde{y}^{2}\tilde{A}_{4}\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}+\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}:\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}})\Big(6\tilde{A}_{4}^{\prime\prime}+13\frac{\tilde{A}_{4}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}\Big)\Big\}.

(L42)

Now by using (
L36
), (

), we are ready to calculate collisional integrals (

The first integral in (

) calculates

\displaystyle m_{a}\int f_{a}\Big[(\boldsymbol{A}^{ab}\boldsymbol{c}_{a})^{S}|\boldsymbol{c}_{a}|^{2}+2(\boldsymbol{A}^{ab}\cdot\boldsymbol{c}_{a})\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\Big]d^{3}v

\displaystyle=\rho_{a}\nu_{ab}\frac{p_{a}^{2}}{\rho_{a}^{2}}\Big\{-\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}\frac{4(2T_{a}m_{b}+5T_{b}m_{a})}{(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})}-\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}\frac{6(3T_{a}m_{b}-7T_{b}m_{a})T_{b}m_{a}}{5(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})^{2}}

\displaystyle+\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}\frac{3(T_{a}m_{b}-T_{b}m_{a})T_{b}^{2}m_{a}^{2}}{(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})^{3}}

\displaystyle-\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{a(2)}\frac{4(8T_{a}^{2}m_{b}^{2}+28T_{a}T_{b}m_{a}m_{b}+35T_{b}^{2}m_{a}^{2})}{5(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})^{2}}

\displaystyle+\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{a(4)}\frac{2(8T_{a}^{3}m_{b}^{3}+36T_{a}^{2}T_{b}m_{a}m_{b}^{2}+63T_{a}T_{b}^{2}m_{a}^{2}m_{b}-70T_{b}^{3}m_{a}^{3})}{35(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})^{3}}\Big\}.

The second integral in (
L32
) calculates

\displaystyle=\rho_{a}\nu_{ab}\frac{p_{a}^{2}}{\rho_{a}^{2}}\Big\{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}\frac{4(2T_{a}m_{b}+5T_{b}m_{a})}{T_{a}(m_{b}+m_{a})}+\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}\frac{2(11T_{a}m_{b}-7T_{b}m_{a})T_{b}m_{a}}{5T_{a}(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})(m_{b}+m_{a})}

\displaystyle-\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}\frac{3(23T_{a}m_{b}-7T_{b}m_{a})T_{b}^{2}m_{a}^{2}}{35T_{a}(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})^{2}(m_{b}+m_{a})}

\displaystyle+\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{a(2)}\frac{2(4T_{a}^{2}m_{b}^{2}+21T_{a}T_{b}m_{a}m_{b}+35T_{b}^{2}m_{a}^{2})}{5T_{a}(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})(m_{b}+m_{a})}

\displaystyle-\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{a(4)}\frac{m_{b}(T_{a}m_{b}+7T_{b}m_{a})(4T_{a}m_{b}+19T_{b}m_{a})}{35(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})^{2}(m_{b}+m_{a})}\Big\}.

(L44)

Adding (
L43
) and (
L44
) together then yields collisional contributions

\displaystyle\textrm{Tr}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}}_{ab}^{(4)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\rho_{a}\nu_{ab}\frac{p_{a}^{2}}{\rho_{a}^{2}}\Big\{+\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}\frac{4(2T_{a}m_{b}+5T_{b}m_{a})m_{a}}{(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})(m_{b}+m_{a})}\frac{(T_{b}-T_{a})}{T_{a}}

(L45)

\displaystyle-M_{ab(1)}\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{a(2)}+M_{ab(2)}\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}-N_{ab(1)}\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{a(4)}-N_{ab(2)}\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}\Big\},

with mass-ratio coefficients

\displaystyle M_{ab(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big\{2\big(16T_{a}^{3}m_{a}m_{b}^{2}+12T_{a}^{3}m_{b}^{3}+56T_{a}^{2}T_{b}m_{a}^{2}m_{b}+31T_{a}^{2}T_{b}m_{a}m_{b}^{2}+70T_{a}T_{b}^{2}m_{a}^{3}

\displaystyle\quad+14T_{a}T_{b}^{2}m_{a}^{2}m_{b}-35T_{b}^{3}m_{a}^{3}\big)\Big\}\Big[5T_{a}(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})^{2}(m_{b}+m_{a})\Big]^{-1};

\displaystyle M_{ab(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,\frac{2T_{b}m_{a}(9T_{a}^{2}m_{a}m_{b}-2T_{a}^{2}m_{b}^{2}-21T_{a}T_{b}m_{a}^{2}-25T_{a}T_{b}m_{a}m_{b}+7T_{b}^{2}m_{a}^{2})}{5(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})^{2}T_{a}(m_{b}+m_{a})};

\displaystyle N_{ab(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,\Big\{16T_{a}^{3}m_{a}m_{b}^{3}+12T_{a}^{3}m_{b}^{4}+72T_{a}^{2}T_{b}m_{a}^{2}m_{b}^{2}+21T_{a}^{2}T_{b}m_{a}m_{b}^{3}+126T_{a}T_{b}^{2}m_{a}^{3}m_{b}

\displaystyle\quad-54T_{a}T_{b}^{2}m_{a}^{2}m_{b}^{2}-140T_{b}^{3}m_{a}^{4}-273T_{b}^{3}m_{a}^{3}m_{b}\Big\}\Big[35(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})^{3}(m_{b}+m_{a})\Big]^{-1};

\displaystyle N_{ab(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,\frac{3T_{b}^{2}m_{a}^{2}(35T_{a}^{2}m_{a}m_{b}+12T_{a}^{2}m_{b}^{2}-35T_{a}T_{b}m_{a}^{2}-51T_{a}T_{b}m_{a}m_{b}+7T_{b}^{2}m_{a}^{2})}{35(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})^{3}T_{a}(m_{b}+m_{a})}.

(L46)

For a particular case of small temperature differences between species the mass-ratio coefficients simplify into

\displaystyle M_{ab(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2(35m_{a}^{2}+35m_{a}m_{b}+12m_{b}^{2})}{5(m_{b}+m_{a})^{2}};\qquad M_{ab(2)}=\frac{4m_{a}(7m_{a}+m_{b})}{5(m_{b}+m_{a})^{2}};

\displaystyle N_{ab(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{140m_{a}^{3}+7m_{a}^{2}m_{b}-25m_{a}m_{b}^{2}-12m_{b}^{3}}{35(m_{b}+m_{a})^{3}};\qquad N_{ab(2)}=\frac{12m_{a}^{2}(7m_{a}-3m_{b})}{35(m_{b}+m_{a})^{3}},

(L47)

and for self-collissions
M_{aa(1)}=41/5
;
M_{aa(2)}=8/5
;
N_{aa(1)}=11/28
;
N_{aa(2)}=6/35
.

L.7
Exchange rates
\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}}_{a}^{(4)}\,{}^{\prime}

Applying trace at (
L45
) yields scalar

\textrm{Tr}\textrm{Tr}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}}_{ab}^{(4)}=\rho_{a}\nu_{ab}\frac{p_{a}^{2}}{\rho_{a}^{2}}\Big\{+3\frac{4(2T_{a}m_{b}+5T_{b}m_{a})m_{a}}{(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})(m_{b}+m_{a})}\frac{(T_{b}-T_{a})}{T_{a}}\Big\},

(L48)

and thus

\displaystyle\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}}^{(4)}_{ab}\,{}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\textrm{Tr}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}}^{(4)}_{ab}-\frac{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}}{3}\textrm{Tr}\textrm{Tr}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}}^{(4)}_{ab}

(L49)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\rho_{a}\nu_{ab}\frac{p_{a}^{2}}{\rho_{a}^{2}}\Big[-M_{ab(1)}\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{a(2)}+M_{ab(2)}\tilde{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(2)}-N_{ab(1)}\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{a(4)}-N_{ab(2)}\hat{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{h}}}}^{b(4)}\Big].

Finally, introducing summation over all “b” species and rewritten with fluid moments

\displaystyle\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}}^{(4)}_{a}\,{}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,\frac{53}{20}\nu_{aa}\frac{p_{a}}{\rho_{a}}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}^{(2)}_{a}-\frac{79}{140}\nu_{aa}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}^{(4)}_{a}+\sum_{b\neq a}\nu_{ab}\Big[-\big(M_{ab(1)}-7N_{ab(1)}\big)\frac{p_{a}}{\rho_{a}}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}^{(2)}_{a}

(L50)

\displaystyle+\big(M_{ab(2)}+7N_{ab(2)}\big)\frac{p_{a}^{2}}{\rho_{a}p_{b}}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}^{(2)}_{b}-N_{ab(1)}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}^{(4)}_{a}-N_{ab(2)}\frac{p_{a}^{2}\rho_{b}}{p_{b}^{2}\rho_{a}}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}^{(4)}_{b}\Big].

It is useful to define (introducing tilde)

\hat{M}_{ab(1)}=M_{ab(1)}-7N_{ab(1)};\qquad\hat{M}_{ab(2)}=M_{ab(2)}+7N_{ab(2)},

(L51)

and the final mass-ratio coefficients are given by (

).

Appendix M
Collisional contributions for scalar
\widetilde{X}^{(4)}

Here we consider perturbation

\chi_{b}=\frac{1}{120}\tilde{h}^{b(4)}\tilde{H}^{b(4)},

(M1)

with Hermite polynomial
\tilde{H}^{(4)}=\tilde{c}^{4}-10\tilde{c}^{2}+15
and Hermite moment
\tilde{h}^{b(4)}=\frac{\rho_{b}}{p_{b}^{2}}\widetilde{X}^{b(4)}
.
The Rosenbluth potentials become

\displaystyle H_{b}(\boldsymbol{v})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{b}\sqrt{\frac{m_{b}}{T_{b}}}\Big\{\frac{1}{\tilde{y}}\textrm{erf}\Big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\Big)+\frac{1}{120}\tilde{h}^{b(4)}(3-\tilde{y}^{2})\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}\Big\};

(M2)

\displaystyle G_{b}(\boldsymbol{v})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n_{b}\sqrt{\frac{T_{b}}{m_{b}}}\Big\{\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}+\big(\tilde{y}+\frac{1}{\tilde{y}}\big)\textrm{erf}\big(\frac{\tilde{y}}{\sqrt{2}}\big)-\frac{1}{60}\tilde{h}^{b(4)}\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}\Big\},

(M3)

and the dynamical friction vector and the diffusion tensor

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{A}^{ab}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\frac{c_{ab}}{m_{a}^{2}}\big(1+\frac{m_{a}}{m_{b}}\big)\frac{n_{b}m_{b}}{T_{b}}\Big\{\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\Big(\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{\tilde{y}^{2}}-\frac{\textrm{erf}(\tilde{y}/\sqrt{2})}{\tilde{y}^{3}}\Big)

(M4)

\displaystyle-\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\frac{\tilde{h}^{b(4)}}{120}\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\big(5-y^{2}\big)e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}\Big\},

\displaystyle\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}^{ab}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\frac{c_{ab}}{m_{a}^{2}}n_{b}\sqrt{\frac{m_{b}}{T_{b}}}\Big\{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}\frac{\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}+\frac{\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}}{\tilde{y}^{2}}\Big(\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime\prime}-\frac{\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}\Big)

(M5)

\displaystyle+\big(\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}-\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\big)\frac{\tilde{h}^{b(4)}}{60}\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}\Big\}.

The perturbation
\chi_{a}=(\tilde{h}^{a(4)}/120)(\alpha^{4}\tilde{y}^{4}-10\alpha^{2}\tilde{y}^{2}+15)
.

M.1
Pressure tensor exchange rates

It is sufficient to consider
\boldsymbol{c}_{a}=\sqrt{T_{a}/m_{a}}\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}
, and so

\displaystyle\big[\boldsymbol{A}^{ab}\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\big]^{S}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\frac{c_{ab}}{m_{a}^{2}}\big(1+\frac{m_{a}}{m_{b}}\big)\frac{n_{b}m_{b}}{T_{b}}\sqrt{\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}}\Big\{2\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\Big(\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}}{\tilde{y}^{2}}-\frac{\textrm{erf}(\tilde{y}/\sqrt{2})}{\tilde{y}^{3}}\Big)

(M6)

\displaystyle-2\alpha\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\frac{\tilde{h}^{b(4)}}{120}\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\big(5-y^{2}\big)e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}\Big\},

which further integrates

\displaystyle m_{a}\int f_{a}\big[\boldsymbol{A}^{ab}\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\big]^{S}d^{3}v

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\rho_{a}\nu_{ab}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}\Big[-2\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}-\tilde{h}^{b(4)}\frac{T_{a}T_{b}^{2}m_{a}}{4(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})^{2}}

(M7)

\displaystyle-\tilde{h}^{a(4)}\frac{m_{b}T_{a}^{2}(T_{a}m_{b}-4T_{b}m_{a})}{20m_{a}(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})^{2}}\Big],

together with

\displaystyle m_{a}\int f_{a}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}_{ab}d^{3}v

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\rho_{a}\nu_{ab}}{m_{a}+m_{b}}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}\Big[\frac{2}{m_{a}}(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})+\tilde{h}^{b(4)}\frac{T_{b}^{2}m_{a}}{20(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})}

(M8)

\displaystyle+\tilde{h}^{a(4)}\frac{m_{b}^{2}T_{a}^{2}}{20m_{a}(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})}\Big].

Adding the last two results together yields collisional contributions

\displaystyle\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}}_{ab}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\rho_{a}\nu_{ab}}{m_{a}+m_{b}}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}\Big[+2(T_{b}-T_{a})-T_{b}\tilde{h}^{b(4)}\frac{T_{b}m_{a}(5T_{a}m_{a}+4T_{a}m_{b}-T_{b}m_{a})}{20(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})^{2}}

(M9)

\displaystyle+T_{a}\tilde{h}^{a(4)}\frac{T_{a}m_{b}(5T_{b}m_{b}+4T_{b}m_{a}-T_{a}m_{b})}{20(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})^{2}}\Big],

which can be written as

\displaystyle\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}}_{ab}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\rho_{a}\nu_{ab}}{m_{a}+m_{b}}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}\Big[+2(T_{b}-T_{a})+P_{ab(1)}T_{a}\tilde{h}^{a(4)}-P_{ab(2)}T_{b}\tilde{h}^{b(4)}\Big],

(M10)

with mass-ratio coefficients

\displaystyle P_{ab(1)}=\frac{T_{a}m_{b}(5T_{b}m_{b}+4T_{b}m_{a}-T_{a}m_{b})}{20(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})^{2}};\qquad P_{ab(2)}=\frac{T_{b}m_{a}(5T_{a}m_{a}+4T_{a}m_{b}-T_{b}m_{a})}{20(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})^{2}},

(M11)

or for the particular case of small temperature differences

\displaystyle P_{ab(1)}=\frac{m_{b}}{5(m_{b}+m_{a})};\qquad P_{ab(2)}=\frac{m_{a}}{5(m_{b}+m_{a})}.

(M12)

The pressure tensor exchange rates (
M10
) are rewritten to fluid variables according to

\displaystyle\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}}_{ab}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\rho_{a}\nu_{ab}}{m_{a}+m_{b}}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}\Big[+2(T_{b}-T_{a})+P_{ab(1)}\frac{\rho_{a}}{n_{a}p_{a}}\widetilde{X}^{(4)}_{a}-P_{ab(2)}\frac{\rho_{b}}{n_{b}p_{b}}\widetilde{X}^{(4)}_{b}\Big].

(M13)

The energy exchange rates then become

\displaystyle Q_{ab}=\frac{1}{2}\textrm{Tr}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}}_{ab}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\rho_{a}\nu_{ab}}{(m_{a}+m_{b})}\Big[+3(T_{b}-T_{a})+\frac{3}{2}P_{ab(1)}\frac{\rho_{a}}{n_{a}p_{a}}\widetilde{X}^{(4)}_{a}-\frac{3}{2}P_{ab(2)}\frac{\rho_{b}}{n_{b}p_{b}}\widetilde{X}^{(4)}_{b}\Big],

(M14)

and collisional contributions for the stress-tensor are

\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}}_{ab}^{(2)}\,{}^{\prime}=\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}}_{ab}^{(2)}-\frac{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}}{3}\textrm{Tr}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}}_{ab}^{(2)}=0.

(M15)

The scalar perturbations
\widetilde{X}^{(4)}_{a}
&
\widetilde{X}^{(4)}_{b}
thus do not modify the
\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}}_{ab}^{(2)}\,{}^{\prime}
,
however they enter the conservation of energy. The final model uses
\hat{P}_{ab(1)}=(3/2)P_{ab(1)}
and

\hat{P}_{ab(2)}=(3/2)P_{ab(2)}
, and the result is written in Section
VII.1
, equation (
140
).
The result is also shown in the Discussion, equation (
177
).

M.2
4th-order moment exchange rates

It is straightforward to calculate

\displaystyle\big(\boldsymbol{A}^{ab}\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\big)^{S}|\boldsymbol{c}_{a}|^{2}+2\big(\boldsymbol{A}^{ab}\cdot\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\big)\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\boldsymbol{c}_{a}

\displaystyle=2\frac{c_{ab}}{m_{a}^{2}}\big(1+\frac{m_{a}}{m_{b}}\big)\frac{n_{b}m_{b}}{T_{b}}\Big(\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}\Big)^{3/2}\alpha^{3}\Big\{4\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\Big(\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}-\frac{\textrm{erf}(\tilde{y}/\sqrt{2})}{\tilde{y}}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad-4\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{y}^{2}(5-\tilde{y}^{2})\frac{\tilde{h}^{b(4)}}{120}\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}\Big\},

(M16)

together with

\displaystyle(\textrm{Tr}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}^{ab})\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\boldsymbol{c}_{a}+\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}^{ab}|\boldsymbol{c}_{a}|^{2}+2\big[(\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}^{ab}\cdot\boldsymbol{c}_{a})\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\big]^{S}

\displaystyle=2\frac{c_{ab}}{m_{a}^{2}}n_{b}\sqrt{\frac{m_{b}}{T_{b}}}\frac{T_{a}}{m_{a}}\alpha^{2}\Big\{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}\tilde{y}\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime}+\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\Big(6\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime\prime}+\frac{\tilde{A}_{1}^{\prime}}{\tilde{y}}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\Big[\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}\tilde{y}^{2}+\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}\tilde{\boldsymbol{y}}(7-6\tilde{y}^{2})\Big]\frac{\tilde{h}^{b(4)}}{60}\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}e^{-\tilde{y}^{2}/2}\Big\},

(M17)

and integrate

\displaystyle m_{a}\int f_{a}\Big[(\boldsymbol{A}^{ab}\boldsymbol{c}_{a})^{S}|\boldsymbol{c}_{a}|^{2}+2(\boldsymbol{A}^{ab}\cdot\boldsymbol{c}_{a})\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\Big]d^{3}v

\displaystyle=\rho_{a}\nu_{ab}\frac{p_{a}^{2}}{\rho_{a}^{2}}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}\Big\{-\frac{4(2T_{a}m_{b}+5T_{b}m_{a})}{(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})}+\tilde{h}^{b(4)}\frac{m_{a}^{2}T_{b}^{2}(2T_{a}m_{b}-5T_{b}m_{a})}{2(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})^{3}}

\displaystyle\quad+\tilde{h}^{a(4)}\frac{2T_{a}^{3}m_{b}^{3}+9T_{a}^{2}T_{b}m_{a}m_{b}^{2}+72T_{a}T_{b}^{2}m_{a}^{2}m_{b}-40T_{b}^{3}m_{a}^{3}}{30(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})^{3}}\Big\},

(M18)

together with

\displaystyle m_{a}\int f_{a}\Big[(\textrm{Tr}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}^{ab})\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\boldsymbol{c}_{a}+\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}^{ab}|\boldsymbol{c}_{a}|^{2}+2\big((\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{D}}}^{ab}\cdot\boldsymbol{c}_{a})\boldsymbol{c}_{a}\big)^{S}\Big]d^{3}v

\displaystyle=\rho_{a}\nu_{ab}\frac{p_{a}^{2}}{\rho_{a}^{2}}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}\Big\{\frac{4(2T_{a}m_{b}+5T_{b}m_{a})}{T_{a}(m_{b}+m_{a})}-\tilde{h}^{b(4)}\frac{T_{b}^{2}m_{a}^{2}(2T_{a}m_{b}-T_{b}m_{a})}{2T_{a}(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})^{2}(m_{b}+m_{a})}

\displaystyle\quad-\tilde{h}^{a(4)}\frac{m_{b}(2T_{a}^{2}m_{b}^{2}+T_{a}T_{b}m_{a}m_{b}+44T_{b}^{2}m_{a}^{2})}{30(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})^{2}(m_{b}+m_{a})}\Big\}.

(M19)

Adding last two results together then yields collisional contributions

\displaystyle\textrm{Tr}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}}_{ab}^{(4)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\rho_{a}\nu_{ab}\frac{p_{a}^{2}}{\rho_{a}^{2}}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}\Big\{+S_{ab(0)}\frac{(T_{b}-T_{a})}{T_{a}}-S_{ab(1)}\tilde{h}^{a(4)}-S_{ab(2)}\tilde{h}^{b(4)}\Big\},

(M20)

with mass-ratio coefficients

\displaystyle S_{ab(0)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{4m_{a}(2T_{a}m_{b}+5T_{b}m_{a})}{(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})(m_{b}+m_{a})};

\displaystyle S_{ab(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,\Big\{m_{a}\big(2T_{a}^{3}m_{b}^{3}+9T_{a}^{2}T_{b}m_{a}m_{b}^{2}+6T_{a}^{2}T_{b}m_{b}^{3}+72T_{a}T_{b}^{2}m_{a}^{2}m_{b}+27T_{a}T_{b}^{2}m_{a}m_{b}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-40T_{b}^{3}m_{a}^{3}-84T_{b}^{3}m_{a}^{2}m_{b}\big)\Big\}\Big[30(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})^{3}(m_{b}+m_{a})\Big]^{-1};

\displaystyle S_{ab(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\,\frac{T_{b}^{2}m_{a}^{3}(2T_{a}^{2}m_{b}-5T_{a}T_{b}m_{a}-6T_{a}T_{b}m_{b}+T_{b}^{2}m_{a})}{2T_{a}(T_{a}m_{b}+T_{b}m_{a})^{3}(m_{b}+m_{a})}.

(M21)

For the particular case of small temperature differences

\displaystyle S_{ab(0)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{4m_{a}(5m_{a}+2m_{b})}{(m_{b}+m_{a})^{2}};

\displaystyle S_{ab(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2m_{a}(10m_{a}^{2}-7m_{a}m_{b}-2m_{b}^{2})}{15(m_{b}+m_{a})^{3}};\qquad S_{ab(2)}=\frac{2m_{a}^{3}}{(m_{b}+m_{a})^{3}},

(M22)

and for self-collisions
S_{aa(1)}=1/60
and
S_{aa(2)}=1/4
.
Applying trace at (
M20
) and changing to fluid moments yields

\textrm{Tr}\textrm{Tr}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}}_{ab}^{(4)}=3\nu_{ab}\frac{p_{a}^{2}}{\rho_{a}}\Big\{+S_{ab(0)}\frac{(T_{b}-T_{a})}{T_{a}}-S_{ab(1)}\frac{\rho_{a}}{p_{a}^{2}}\widetilde{X}^{(4)}_{a}-S_{ab(2)}\frac{\rho_{b}}{p_{b}^{2}}\widetilde{X}^{(4)}_{b}\Big\},

(M23)

and collisional contributions for the stress-tensor
\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}^{(4)}_{a}
are

\displaystyle\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}}^{(4)}_{ab}\,{}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\textrm{Tr}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}}^{(4)}_{ab}-\frac{\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{I}}}}{3}\textrm{Tr}\textrm{Tr}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}}^{(4)}_{ab}=0.

(M24)

M.3
Collisional contributions
\widetilde{Q}^{(4)}_{a}\,{}^{\prime}

Collisional contributions for the evolution equation
\widetilde{X}^{(4)}_{a}
, equation (
C33
), then become

\displaystyle\widetilde{Q}^{(4)}_{ab}\,{}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\textrm{Tr}\textrm{Tr}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}}^{(4)}_{ab}-20\frac{p_{a}}{\rho_{a}}Q_{ab}

(M25)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\nu_{ab}\Big\{+\frac{p_{a}^{2}}{\rho_{a}}\frac{(T_{b}-T_{a})}{T_{a}}\Big(3S_{ab(0)}-\frac{60m_{a}}{m_{a}+m_{b}}\Big)-\widetilde{X}^{(4)}_{a}\Big(3S_{ab(1)}+\frac{30m_{a}}{m_{a}+m_{b}}P_{ab(1)}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{p_{a}^{2}\rho_{b}}{p_{b}^{2}\rho_{a}}\widetilde{X}^{(4)}_{b}\Big(3S_{ab(2)}-\frac{30m_{a}}{(m_{a}+m_{b})}\frac{T_{b}}{T_{a}}P_{ab(2)}\Big)\Big\}.

It is useful to define

\displaystyle\hat{S}_{ab(0)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\Big(3S_{ab(0)}-\frac{60m_{a}}{m_{a}+m_{b}}\Big);

\displaystyle\hat{S}_{ab(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 3S_{ab(1)}+\frac{30m_{a}}{m_{a}+m_{b}}P_{ab(1)};

\displaystyle\hat{S}_{ab(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\Big(3S_{ab(2)}-\frac{30m_{a}}{(m_{a}+m_{b})}\frac{T_{b}}{T_{a}}P_{ab(2)}\Big),

(M26)

and the final model then reads

\displaystyle\widetilde{Q}^{(4)}_{ab}\,{}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\textrm{Tr}\textrm{Tr}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{Q}}}^{(4)}_{ab}-20\frac{p_{a}}{\rho_{a}}Q_{ab}

(M27)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\nu_{ab}\Big\{-\frac{p_{a}^{2}}{\rho_{a}}\frac{(T_{b}-T_{a})}{T_{a}}\hat{S}_{ab(0)}-\widetilde{X}^{(4)}_{a}\hat{S}_{ab(1)}+\frac{p_{a}^{2}\rho_{b}}{p_{b}^{2}\rho_{a}}\widetilde{X}^{(4)}_{b}\hat{S}_{ab(2)}\Big\},

with mass-ratio coefficients given by (
143
).

Appendix N
Coupling of two species

Here we would like to emphasize the usefullness of the mutli-fluid formulation, which makes calculation of transport coefficients
straightforward. We consider two species with indices “a” and “b”. Evolution equations for heat fluxes “a” become

\displaystyle\frac{d_{a}}{dt}\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}_{a}+\Omega_{a}\boldsymbol{\hat{b}}\times\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}_{a}+\frac{5}{2}p_{a}\nabla\Big(\frac{p_{a}}{\rho_{a}}\Big)=-\Big[2\nu_{aa}+\nu_{ab}\hat{D}_{ab(1)}\Big]\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}_{a}+\nu_{ab}\hat{D}_{ab(2)}\frac{\rho_{a}}{\rho_{b}}\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}_{b}

\displaystyle\qquad+\Big[\frac{3}{70}\nu_{aa}+\nu_{ab}\hat{E}_{ab(1)}\Big]\frac{\rho_{a}}{p_{a}}\vec{\boldsymbol{X}}^{(5)}_{a}-\nu_{ab}\hat{E}_{ab(2)}\frac{\rho_{a}}{p_{b}}\vec{\boldsymbol{X}}^{(5)}_{b}-p_{a}\nu_{ab}(\boldsymbol{u}_{b}-\boldsymbol{u}_{a})\hat{U}_{ab(1)};

(N1)

\displaystyle\frac{d_{a}}{dt}\vec{\boldsymbol{X}}^{(5)}_{a}+\Omega_{a}\boldsymbol{\hat{b}}\times\vec{\boldsymbol{X}}^{(5)}_{a}+70\frac{p_{a}^{2}}{\rho_{a}}\nabla\Big(\frac{p_{a}}{\rho_{a}}\Big)=-\Big[\frac{76}{5}\nu_{aa}+\nu_{ab}\hat{F}_{ab(1)}\Big]\frac{p_{a}}{\rho_{a}}\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}_{a}+\nu_{ab}\hat{F}_{ab(2)}\frac{p_{a}}{\rho_{b}}\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}_{b}

\displaystyle\qquad-\Big[\frac{3}{35}\nu_{aa}+\nu_{ab}\hat{G}_{ab(1)}\Big]\vec{\boldsymbol{X}}^{(5)}_{a}-\nu_{ab}\hat{G}_{ab(2)}\frac{p_{a}}{p_{b}}\vec{\boldsymbol{X}}^{(5)}_{b}-\frac{p_{a}^{2}}{\rho_{a}}\nu_{ab}(\boldsymbol{u}_{b}-\boldsymbol{u}_{a})\hat{U}_{ab(2)},

(N2)

together with evolution equations for heat fluxes “b”

\displaystyle\frac{d_{b}}{dt}\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}_{b}+\Omega_{b}\boldsymbol{\hat{b}}\times\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}_{b}+\frac{5}{2}p_{b}\nabla\Big(\frac{p_{b}}{\rho_{b}}\Big)=-\Big[2\nu_{bb}+\nu_{ba}\hat{D}_{ba(1)}\Big]\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}_{b}+\nu_{ba}\hat{D}_{ba(2)}\frac{\rho_{b}}{\rho_{a}}\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}_{a}

\displaystyle\qquad+\Big[\frac{3}{70}\nu_{bb}+\nu_{ba}\hat{E}_{ba(1)}\Big]\frac{\rho_{b}}{p_{b}}\vec{\boldsymbol{X}}^{(5)}_{b}-\nu_{ba}\hat{E}_{ba(2)}\frac{\rho_{b}}{p_{a}}\vec{\boldsymbol{X}}^{(5)}_{a}+p_{b}\nu_{ba}(\boldsymbol{u}_{b}-\boldsymbol{u}_{a})\hat{U}_{ba(1)};

(N3)

\displaystyle\frac{d_{b}}{dt}\vec{\boldsymbol{X}}^{(5)}_{b}+\Omega_{b}\boldsymbol{\hat{b}}\times\vec{\boldsymbol{X}}^{(5)}_{b}+70\frac{p_{b}^{2}}{\rho_{b}}\nabla\Big(\frac{p_{b}}{\rho_{b}}\Big)=-\Big[\frac{76}{5}\nu_{bb}+\nu_{ba}\hat{F}_{ba(1)}\Big]\frac{p_{b}}{\rho_{b}}\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}_{b}+\nu_{ba}\hat{F}_{ba(2)}\frac{p_{b}}{\rho_{a}}\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}_{a}

\displaystyle\qquad-\Big[\frac{3}{35}\nu_{bb}+\nu_{ba}\hat{G}_{ba(1)}\Big]\vec{\boldsymbol{X}}^{(5)}_{b}-\nu_{ba}\hat{G}_{ba(2)}\frac{p_{b}}{p_{a}}\vec{\boldsymbol{X}}^{(5)}_{a}+\frac{p_{b}^{2}}{\rho_{b}}\nu_{ba}(\boldsymbol{u}_{b}-\boldsymbol{u}_{a})\hat{U}_{ba(2)},

(N4)

where for similar temperatures the mass-ratio coefficients are given by (
27
), (
28
)
and for arbitrary temperatures by (
19
), (
21
).
The system is fully specified and after precribing
quasi-static approximation it can be solved. Unfortunatelly, the general analytic solution is too long to write down, even for the unmagnetized case.
It is beneficial to consider a specific example. Nevertheless, the above system is a very powerfull tool, which allows one to obtain
transport coefficients between two different species, being it a two ion plasma, or a precise solutions for a specific ion-electron plasma without
neglecting
m_{e}/m_{i}
.

Similarly, the viscosity between two species is described by evolution equations for viscosity-tensors of species “a”

\displaystyle\frac{d_{a}}{dt}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}^{(2)}_{a}+\Omega_{a}\big(\boldsymbol{\hat{b}}\times\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}^{(2)}_{a}\big)^{S}+p_{a}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{W}}}_{a}=-\,\frac{21}{10}\nu_{aa}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}_{a}^{(2)}+\frac{9}{70}\nu_{aa}\frac{\rho_{a}}{p_{a}}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}_{a}^{(4)}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\rho_{a}\nu_{ab}}{m_{a}+m_{b}}\Big[-\hat{K}_{ab(1)}\frac{1}{n_{a}}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}_{a}^{(2)}+\hat{K}_{ab(2)}\frac{1}{n_{b}}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}_{b}^{(2)}+L_{ab(1)}\frac{\rho_{a}}{n_{a}p_{a}}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}_{a}^{(4)}-L_{ab(2)}\frac{\rho_{b}}{n_{b}p_{b}}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}_{b}^{(4)}\Big];

(N5)

\displaystyle\frac{d_{a}}{dt}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}^{(4)}_{a}+\Omega_{a}\big(\boldsymbol{\hat{b}}\times\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}^{(4)}_{a}\big)^{S}+7\frac{p_{a}^{2}}{\rho_{a}}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{W}}}_{a}=-\,\frac{53}{20}\nu_{aa}\frac{p_{a}}{\rho_{a}}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}^{(2)}_{a}-\frac{79}{140}\nu_{aa}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}^{(4)}_{a}

\displaystyle\qquad+\nu_{ab}\Big[-\hat{M}_{ab(1)}\frac{p_{a}}{\rho_{a}}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}^{(2)}_{a}+\hat{M}_{ab(2)}\frac{p_{a}^{2}}{\rho_{a}p_{b}}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}^{(2)}_{b}-N_{ab(1)}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}^{(4)}_{a}-N_{ab(2)}\frac{p_{a}^{2}\rho_{b}}{p_{b}^{2}\rho_{a}}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}^{(4)}_{b}\Big],

(N6)

together with evolution equations for viscosity-tensors of species “b”

\displaystyle\frac{d_{b}}{dt}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}^{(2)}_{b}+\Omega_{b}\big(\boldsymbol{\hat{b}}\times\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}^{(2)}_{b}\big)^{S}+p_{b}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{W}}}_{b}=-\,\frac{21}{10}\nu_{bb}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}_{b}^{(2)}+\frac{9}{70}\nu_{bb}\frac{\rho_{b}}{p_{b}}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}_{b}^{(4)}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\rho_{b}\nu_{ba}}{m_{a}+m_{b}}\Big[-\hat{K}_{ba(1)}\frac{1}{n_{b}}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}_{b}^{(2)}+\hat{K}_{ba(2)}\frac{1}{n_{a}}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}_{a}^{(2)}+L_{ba(1)}\frac{\rho_{b}}{n_{b}p_{b}}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}_{b}^{(4)}-L_{ba(2)}\frac{\rho_{a}}{n_{a}p_{a}}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}_{a}^{(4)}\Big];

(N7)

\displaystyle\frac{d_{b}}{dt}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}^{(4)}_{b}+\Omega_{b}\big(\boldsymbol{\hat{b}}\times\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}^{(4)}_{b}\big)^{S}+7\frac{p_{b}^{2}}{\rho_{b}}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{W}}}_{b}=-\,\frac{53}{20}\nu_{bb}\frac{p_{b}}{\rho_{b}}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}^{(2)}_{b}-\frac{79}{140}\nu_{bb}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}^{(4)}_{b}

\displaystyle\qquad+\nu_{ba}\Big[-\hat{M}_{ba(1)}\frac{p_{b}}{\rho_{b}}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}^{(2)}_{b}+\hat{M}_{ba(2)}\frac{p_{b}^{2}}{\rho_{b}p_{a}}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}^{(2)}_{a}-N_{ba(1)}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}^{(4)}_{b}-N_{ba(2)}\frac{p_{b}^{2}\rho_{a}}{p_{a}^{2}\rho_{b}}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}^{(4)}_{a}\Big].

(N8)

Here the heat fluxes (
N1
)-(
N4
) and viscosities (
N5
)-(
N8
) are
de-coupled, but one can consider more precise solutions with coupling between heat fluxes and viscosities, similarly
to Section
VI
.

N.1
Protons and alpha particles (unmagnetized)

As an example, we consider collisions between protons and alpha particles (fully ionized Helium with proton mass 4).
Protons will be “a” species and alpha particles will be “b” species. For the ion coefficients, collisions with electrons are neglected in
an analogous fashion to
Braginskii 1965
.
By prescribing mass
m_{b}=4m_{a}
, the mass-ratio coefficients with equal temperatures
T_{a}=T_{b}
become

\displaystyle\hat{D}_{ab(1)}=\frac{499}{125};\qquad\hat{D}_{ab(2)}=\frac{396}{125};\qquad\hat{E}_{ab(1)}=\frac{87}{875};\qquad\hat{E}_{ab(2)}=\frac{9}{175};\qquad\hat{U}_{ab(1)}=\frac{6}{5};

\displaystyle\hat{F}_{ab(1)}=\frac{7624}{125};\qquad\hat{F}_{ab(2)}=\frac{4848}{125};\qquad\hat{G}_{ab(1)}=-\,\frac{171}{125};\qquad\hat{G}_{ab(2)}=\frac{12}{25};\qquad\hat{U}_{ab(2)}=24,

\displaystyle\hat{D}_{ba(1)}=\frac{2011}{500};\qquad\hat{D}_{ba(2)}=\frac{117}{250};\qquad\hat{E}_{ba(1)}=\frac{897}{14000};\qquad\hat{E}_{ba(2)}=\frac{9}{700};\qquad\hat{U}_{ba(1)}=\frac{3}{10};

\displaystyle\hat{F}_{ba(1)}=\frac{979}{50};\qquad\hat{F}_{ba(2)}=\frac{1383}{125};\qquad\hat{G}_{ba(1)}=\frac{8907}{7000};\qquad\hat{G}_{ba(2)}=\frac{3}{10};\qquad\hat{U}_{ba(2)}=\frac{39}{5}.

(N9)

By specifying charges
Z_{a}=1
;
Z_{b}=2
, the four different collisional frequencies are related by

\nu_{ba}=\frac{\rho_{a}}{\rho_{b}}\nu_{ab};\qquad\nu_{ab}=8\frac{n_{b}}{n_{a}}\sqrt{\frac{2}{5}}\nu_{aa};\qquad\nu_{bb}=8\frac{n_{b}}{n_{a}}\nu_{aa},

(N10)

and we chose
\nu_{aa}
as the reference frequency. Furthermore, applying the charge neutrality
n_{a}+2n_{b}=n_{e}
we choose as
a reference normalized density
N_{a}\equiv n_{a}/n_{e}
and express
n_{b}/n_{e}=(1-N_{a})/2
. We also prescribe
\nabla T_{a}=\nabla T_{b}
.

Then solving the system yields (parallel) thermal heat fluxes
\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}_{a}^{T}=-\kappa_{a}\nabla T_{a}
;
\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}_{b}^{T}=-\kappa_{b}\nabla T_{a}
with
thermal conductivities

\kappa_{a}=\frac{T_{a}n_{a}}{m_{a}\nu_{aa}}\hat{\kappa}_{a};\qquad\kappa_{b}=\frac{T_{a}n_{b}}{m_{b}\nu_{bb}}\hat{\kappa}_{b},

(N11)

and with normalized fully analytic values

\displaystyle\hat{\kappa}_{a}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle N_{a}\bigg\{\Big(-\frac{17989001}{10557600}\sqrt{10}+\frac{292708195}{54054912}\Big)N_{a}^{3}+\Big(\frac{2129490299}{675686400}\sqrt{10}-\frac{1032644005}{108109824}\Big)N_{a}^{2}

(N12)

\displaystyle\qquad+\Big(-\frac{98252949}{45045760}\sqrt{10}+\frac{8035835}{1689216}\Big)N_{a}+\frac{51625}{70384}\sqrt{10}+\frac{3425}{140768}\bigg\}/\triangle_{1};

\displaystyle\hat{\kappa}_{b}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 32(1-N_{a})\bigg\{\frac{125}{1024}+\Big(\frac{128513167}{2162196480}\sqrt{10}-\frac{166007075}{864878592}\Big)N_{a}^{3}

(N13)

\displaystyle+\Big(-\frac{67953383}{540549120}\sqrt{10}+\frac{386788475}{864878592}\Big)N_{a}^{2}+\Big(\frac{15671599}{216219648}\sqrt{10}-\frac{1540025}{4504576}\Big)N_{a}\bigg\}/\triangle_{1};

\displaystyle\triangle_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big[1+\Big(-\frac{722521001}{563072000}\sqrt{10}+\frac{14274588957}{3519200000}\Big)N_{a}^{4}+\Big(\frac{1043512703}{337843200}\sqrt{10}-\frac{8606493541}{879800000}\Big)N_{a}^{3}

(N14)

\displaystyle\qquad+\Big(-\frac{23828129}{8798000}\sqrt{10}+\frac{15644893541}{1759600000}\Big)N_{a}^{2}+\Big(\frac{23828129}{26394000}\sqrt{10}-4\Big)N_{a}\Big],

or with numerical values

\displaystyle\hat{\kappa}_{a}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle N_{a}\big[2.3438+0.02684N_{a}^{3}+0.4144N_{a}^{2}-2.1404N_{a}\big]/\triangle_{1};

(N15)

\displaystyle\hat{\kappa}_{b}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 32(1-N_{a})\big[0.1221-0.003988N_{a}^{3}+0.04968N_{a}^{2}-0.1127N_{a}\big]/\triangle_{1};

(N16)

\displaystyle\triangle_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 1-0.001559N_{a}^{4}-0.01485N_{a}^{3}+0.3266N_{a}^{2}-1.1451N_{a}.

(N17)

Note that
n_{a}/\nu_{aa}
is independent of
n_{a}
, and that is why definitions (
N11
) were chosen.
For the “b” species (alpha particles), the results are written in a form
so that it is easy to use
32n_{b}/(\nu_{bb}m_{b})=n_{a}/(\nu_{aa}m_{a})
.
As a double check, prescribing

\displaystyle N_{a}=1;\quad=>\qquad\kappa_{a}=\frac{T_{a}n_{a}}{\nu_{aa}m_{a}}\frac{125}{32};\qquad\kappa_{b}=0;

\displaystyle N_{a}=0;\quad=>\qquad\kappa_{a}=0;\qquad\kappa_{b}=\frac{T_{a}n_{b}}{\nu_{bb}m_{b}}\frac{125}{32},

(N18)

as it should be. In general, thermal conductivities of a single ion plasmas compare
as
\kappa_{a}/\kappa_{b}=\sqrt{m_{b}/m_{a}}(Z_{b}/Z_{a})^{4}
. In our case, thermal conductivity of pure alpha particles is
32 times smaller than of pure protons. The thermal conductivities
\hat{\kappa}_{a},\hat{\kappa}_{b}

are plotted in the left panel of Figure
7
.

The frictional heat fluxes read

\displaystyle\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}_{a}^{u}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-T_{a}n_{e}(\boldsymbol{u}_{b}-\boldsymbol{u}_{a})\beta_{0a};\qquad\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}_{b}^{u}=-T_{a}n_{e}(\boldsymbol{u}_{b}-\boldsymbol{u}_{a})\beta_{0b};

(N19)

\displaystyle\beta_{0a}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle N_{a}(1-N_{a})\bigg\{\Big(-\frac{150058601}{43990000}+\frac{1522393}{1407680}\sqrt{10}\Big)N_{a}^{3}

(N20)

\displaystyle+\Big(+\frac{258658601}{43990000}-\frac{199422}{109975}\sqrt{10}\Big)N_{a}^{2}+\Big(-\frac{16290}{4399}+\frac{99711}{109975}\sqrt{10}\Big)N_{a}+\frac{5430}{4399}\bigg\}/\triangle_{1};

\displaystyle\beta_{0b}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle N_{a}(1-N_{a})\bigg\{\frac{7351}{1407680}\sqrt{10}+\Big(-\frac{54551}{22522880}\sqrt{10}+\frac{264247}{35192000}\Big)N_{a}^{3}

(N21)

\displaystyle+\Big(\frac{289783}{22522880}\sqrt{10}-\frac{2663863}{70384000}\Big)N_{a}^{2}+\Big(-\frac{22053}{1407680}\sqrt{10}+\frac{2663863}{140768000}\Big)N_{a}\bigg\}/\triangle_{1},

where the denominator
\triangle_{1}
is identical to (
N14
), and with numerical values

\displaystyle\beta_{0a}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle N_{a}(1-N_{a})\big[1.2344+0.008776N_{a}^{3}+0.1457N_{a}^{2}-0.8360N_{a}\big]/\triangle_{1};

(N22)

\displaystyle\beta_{0b}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle N_{a}(1-N_{a})\big[0.01651-0.0001504N_{a}^{3}+0.002839N_{a}^{2}-0.03062N_{a}\big]/\triangle_{1}.

(N23)

In both limits
N_{a}=0,1
the frictional heat fluxes disappear. The frictional heat fluxes are plotted in the middle and right
panels of Figure
7
.

Figure 7:
Left panel: proton thermal conductivity
\hat{\kappa}_{a}
(red) given by (
N15
),
and alpha-particles thermal conductivity
\hat{\kappa}_{b}
(black) given by (
N16
).
Middle panel: proton frictional heat flux given by
\beta_{0a}
(
N22
).
Right panel: alpha-particle frictional heat flux given by
\beta_{0b}
(
N23
). Note the surprising change of sign
of
\beta_{0b}
for
N_{a}>0.57
. We have verified that the same effect is present in the simplified 13-moment model of

Burgers 1969
-
Schunk 1977
.

Viscosities

One first calculates the required viscosity mass-ratio coefficients, which for protons (“a”) and alpha particles (“b”) become

\displaystyle\hat{K}_{ab(1)}=\frac{398}{25};\qquad\hat{K}_{ab(2)}=\frac{32}{25};\qquad{L}_{ab(1)}=\frac{228}{175};\qquad{L}_{ab(2)}=\frac{12}{175};

\displaystyle\hat{M}_{ab(1)}=\frac{934}{125};\qquad\hat{M}_{ab(2)}=\frac{32}{125};\qquad{N}_{ab(1)}=-\frac{8}{35};\qquad{N}_{ab(2)}=-\frac{12}{875};

\displaystyle\hat{K}_{ba(1)}=\frac{323}{100};\qquad\hat{K}_{ba(2)}=\frac{68}{25};\qquad{L}_{ba(1)}=\frac{93}{700};\qquad{L}_{ba(2)}=\frac{48}{175};

\displaystyle\hat{M}_{ba(1)}=-\frac{368}{125};\qquad\hat{M}_{ba(2)}=\frac{1424}{125};\qquad{N}_{ba(1)}=\frac{256}{125};\qquad{N}_{ba(2)}=\frac{192}{175},

(N24)

and which enter evolution equations (
N5
)-(
N8
). For an unmagnetized plasma,
quasi-static solution of these equations then yields viscosity-tensors

\displaystyle\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}^{(2)}_{a}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{p_{a}}{\nu_{aa}}\big[\hat{\eta}_{aa}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{W}}}_{a}+\hat{\eta}_{ab}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{W}}}_{b}\big];

\displaystyle\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}^{(2)}_{b}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{p_{b}}{\nu_{bb}}\big[8\hat{\eta}_{ab}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{W}}}_{a}+\hat{\eta}_{bb}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{W}}}_{b}\big],

(N25)

with numerical values

\displaystyle\hat{\eta}_{aa}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle N_{a}(-0.05464N_{a}^{3}+0.3704N_{a}^{2}-0.7717N_{a}+0.5173)/\triangle;

\displaystyle\hat{\eta}_{ab}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle N_{a}(1-N_{a})(0.001874N_{a}^{2}-0.008142N_{a}+0.01248)/\triangle;

\displaystyle\hat{\eta}_{bb}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 8(1-N_{a})(-0.01150N_{a}^{3}+0.07862N_{a}^{2}-0.1729N_{a}+0.11997)/\triangle;

\displaystyle\triangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 1+0.03923N_{a}^{4}-0.3759N_{a}^{3}+1.2959N_{a}^{2}-1.8953N_{a}.

(N26)

Note that
p_{a}/\nu_{aa}=8p_{b}/\nu_{bb}
and the chosen form (
N25
) emphasizes that the “cross-viscosities”
\hat{\eta}_{ab}
are
directly related. In general, viscosities of a pure single ion species compare as
\eta_{a}/\eta_{b}=\sqrt{m_{a}/m_{b}}(Z_{b}/Z_{a})^{4}
,
so in our case the viscosity of pure alpha particles is 8 times smaller than of pure protons.
We provide only numerical values for solutions (
N26
), nevertheless it can be shown that for
N_{a}=1
the proton viscosity

\hat{\eta}_{aa}=1025/1068
and the same result is obtained for the alpha particle viscosity
\hat{\eta}_{bb}
if
N_{a}=0
.
The “cross-viscosity”
\hat{\eta}_{ab}
becomes zero for both
N_{a}=1
and
N_{a}=0
. Results are plotted in Figure
8
.

Figure 8:
Normalized viscosities of proton & alpha-particle plasma, according to (
N26
). Collisions with electrons are neglected,
in an analogous fasion to Braginskii. Left panel: proton viscosity
\hat{\eta}_{aa}
(red)
and alpha-particle viscosity
\hat{\eta}_{bb}
(black). Right panel: “cross-viscosity”
\hat{\eta}_{ab}
.

N.2
Deuterium & tritium plasma (unmagnetized)

Here we calculate another example of deuterium-tritium plasma, also considered by
Simakov & Molvig 2016b
.
Plasma consisting of deuterium-tritium is probably the most efficient way to achieve
plasma fusion. It is for example being used in the JET machine (see e.g.
Joffrin et al. 2019
)
and it will be used in ITER (www.iter.org/sci/FusionFuels).
Of course, we do not consider peculiar complications
associated with the neoclassical toroidal geometry, our calculation is classical.
Deuterium core consists of one proton and one neutron. Tritium core consists of one proton and two neutrons.
Deuterium will be “a” species and tritium will be “b” species. Collisions with electrons are neglected.
By prescribing
m_{b}=(3/2)m_{a}
, the mass-ratio coefficients with equal temperatures
T_{b}=T_{a}
become

\displaystyle\hat{D}_{ab(1)}=\frac{1989}{500};\qquad\hat{D}_{ab(2)}=\frac{324}{125};\qquad\hat{E}_{ab(1)}=\frac{189}{2000};\qquad\hat{E}_{ab(2)}=\frac{81}{1400};\qquad\hat{U}_{ab(1)}=\frac{9}{10};

\displaystyle\hat{F}_{ab(1)}=\frac{13543}{250};\qquad\hat{F}_{ab(2)}=\frac{5022}{125};\qquad\hat{G}_{ab(1)}=-\,\frac{1373}{1400};\qquad\hat{G}_{ab(2)}=\frac{81}{100};\qquad\hat{U}_{ab(2)}=\frac{99}{5},

\displaystyle\hat{D}_{ba(1)}=\frac{521}{125};\qquad\hat{D}_{ba(2)}=\frac{189}{125};\qquad\hat{E}_{ba(1)}=\frac{78}{875};\qquad\hat{E}_{ba(2)}=\frac{27}{700};\qquad\hat{U}_{ba(1)}=\frac{3}{5};

\displaystyle\hat{F}_{ba(1)}=\frac{5832}{125};\qquad\hat{F}_{ba(2)}=\frac{3672}{125};\qquad\hat{G}_{ba(1)}=-\,\frac{307}{875};\qquad\hat{G}_{ba(2)}=\frac{18}{25};\qquad\hat{U}_{ba(2)}=\frac{72}{5}.

(N27)

Further specifying
Z_{a}=Z_{b}=1
, the collisional frequencies are related by

\nu_{ab}=\frac{n_{b}}{n_{a}}\sqrt{\frac{6}{5}}\nu_{aa};\qquad\nu_{bb}=\frac{n_{b}}{n_{a}}\sqrt{\frac{2}{3}}\nu_{aa},

(N28)

and the charge neutrality
n_{a}+n_{b}=n_{e}
implies
n_{b}/n_{e}=1-N_{a}
, where
N_{a}=n_{a}/n_{e}
.
These mass-ratio coefficients and collisional frequencies are used in the system (
N1
)-(
N4
).
We present quasi-static solutions only for the unmagnetized case, and we assume
\nabla T_{a}=\nabla T_{b}
.
Thermal heat fluxes
\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}_{a}^{T}=-\kappa_{a}\nabla T_{a}
;
\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}_{b}^{T}=-\kappa_{b}\nabla T_{a}
are given by

\kappa_{a}=\frac{T_{a}n_{a}}{m_{a}\nu_{aa}}\hat{\kappa}_{a};\qquad\kappa_{b}=\frac{T_{a}n_{b}}{m_{b}\nu_{bb}}\hat{\kappa}_{b},

(N29)

and with numerical values

\displaystyle\hat{\kappa}_{a}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle N_{a}\big(4.2135-0.009780N_{a}^{3}+0.06292N_{a}^{2}+1.4992N_{a}\big)/\triangle;

\displaystyle\hat{\kappa}_{b}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{3/2}(1-N_{a})\big(3.1894-0.001385N_{a}^{3}+0.04936N_{a}^{2}+0.9845N_{a}\big)/\triangle;

\displaystyle\triangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 1-0.0021475N_{a}^{4}-0.01543N_{a}^{3}+0.01753N_{a}^{2}+0.4761N_{a},

(N30)

where one can also use
\sqrt{3/2}n_{b}/(m_{b}\nu_{bb})=n_{a}/(\nu_{aa}m_{a})
.
The frictional heat fluxes are given by

\displaystyle\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}_{a}^{u}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-T_{a}n_{e}(\boldsymbol{u}_{b}-\boldsymbol{u}_{a})\beta_{0a};\qquad\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}_{b}^{u}=+T_{a}n_{e}(\boldsymbol{u}_{b}-\boldsymbol{u}_{a})\beta_{0b};

(N31)

\displaystyle\beta_{0a}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle N_{a}(1-N_{a})\big[0.81156+0.010099N_{a}^{3}+0.098815N_{a}^{2}+0.50235N_{a}\big]/\triangle;

(N32)

\displaystyle\beta_{0b}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle N_{a}(1-N_{a})\big[0.26178+0.0088461N_{a}^{3}+0.069351N_{a}^{2}+0.24742N_{a}\big]/\triangle.

(N33)

Figure 9:
Left panel: deuterium thermal conductivity
\hat{\kappa}_{a}
(red)
and tritium thermal conductivity
\hat{\kappa}_{b}
(black), given by (
N30
).
Middle panel: deuterium frictional heat flux given by
\beta_{0a}
(
N32
).
Right panel: tritium frictional heat flux given by
\beta_{0b}
(
N33
). Note that the frictional heat fluxes
\vec{\boldsymbol{q}}_{b}^{u}

are defined with opposite signs in (
N31
) and (
N19
).

Viscosities

The required viscosity mass-ratio coefficients for deuterium (“a”) and tritium (“b”) become

\displaystyle\hat{K}_{ab(1)}=\frac{397}{50};\qquad\hat{K}_{ab(2)}=\frac{44}{25};\qquad{L}_{ab(1)}=\frac{207}{350};\qquad{L}_{ab(2)}=\frac{24}{175};

\displaystyle\hat{M}_{ab(1)}=\frac{166}{25};\qquad\hat{M}_{ab(2)}=\frac{184}{125};\qquad{N}_{ab(1)}=\frac{86}{875};\qquad{N}_{ab(2)}=\frac{48}{875};

\displaystyle\hat{K}_{ba(1)}=\frac{124}{25};\qquad\hat{K}_{ba(2)}=\frac{56}{25};\qquad{L}_{ba(1)}=\frac{54}{175};\qquad{L}_{ba(2)}=\frac{36}{175};

\displaystyle\hat{M}_{ba(1)}=\frac{444}{125};\qquad\hat{M}_{ba(2)}=\frac{24}{5};\qquad{N}_{ba(1)}=\frac{702}{875};\qquad{N}_{ba(2)}=\frac{324}{875},

(N34)

and enter evolution equations (
N5
)-(
N8
). For an unmagnetized plasma the solutions
read

\displaystyle\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}^{(2)}_{a}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{p_{a}}{\nu_{aa}}\big[\hat{\eta}_{aa}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{W}}}_{a}+\hat{\eta}_{ab}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{W}}}_{b}\big];

\displaystyle\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{\Pi}}}^{(2)}_{b}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{p_{b}}{\nu_{bb}}\big[\sqrt{\frac{2}{3}}\hat{\eta}_{ab}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{W}}}_{a}+\hat{\eta}_{bb}\bar{\bar{\boldsymbol{W}}}_{b}\big],

(N35)

with numerical values

\displaystyle\hat{\eta}_{aa}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle N_{a}(0.0046589N_{a}^{3}+0.0064481N_{a}^{2}+0.17316N_{a}+0.85048)/\triangle;

\displaystyle\hat{\eta}_{ab}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle N_{a}(1-N_{a})(0.0049729N_{a}^{2}+0.028578N_{a}+0.16621)/\triangle;

\displaystyle\hat{\eta}_{bb}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{2/3}(1-N_{a})(-0.0057061N_{a}^{3}-0.047294N_{a}^{2}-0.10519N_{a}+1.17543)/\triangle;

\displaystyle\triangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 1+0.00017711N_{a}^{4}-0.00044516N_{a}^{3}-0.020987N_{a}^{2}+0.099409N_{a}.

(N36)

The solutions are written in a form so that one can directly use
\sqrt{2/3}p_{b}/\nu_{bb}=p_{a}/\nu_{aa}
, and are plotted in Figure (
10
).
To obtain more precise solutions one should include collisions with electrons (i.e. consider coupling between 3 species). Nevertheless, the
self-collisional values
1025/1068=0.96
will only change to roughly 0.89, see for example equation (
217
),
and the plotted viscosity profiles will not change much.

Figure 10:
Viscosities of deuterium & tritium plasma, according to (
N36
).
Left panel: deuterium viscosity
\hat{\eta}_{aa}
(red)
and tritium viscosity
\hat{\eta}_{bb}
(black). Right panel: “cross-viscosity”
\hat{\eta}_{ab}
.
